Method: 采用受控实验方法，在合成数据集和真实大规模数据集上进行系统探索多模态预训练。具体包括：(i) 解耦语言、视觉理解和视觉生成之间的知识流传递，分析影响模式与不对称性；(ii) 研究数据复杂度对模态协同/竞争的影响，并考察共享注意力、归一化与模态特定前馈层等架构选择；(iii) 对比早期统一（从初始阶段联合训练）、后期对齐与顺序训练的效果，揭示"视觉懒惰"现象；(iv) 推导高效预训练配方。最终通过训练13.5B MoE模型在2T tokens上规模化验证。

Result: 获得四大关键发现：知识流动呈现不同的影响模式与不对称性；数据复杂度决定模态是协同还是竞争，共享注意力与归一化配合模态特定前馈层能促进协同，且这些行为在不同视觉tokenizer设计上泛化；早期统一训练优于后期对齐或顺序训练，延迟整合导致模型依赖语言先验的"视觉懒惰"现象；仅用5%的计算预算即可实现强生成性能的高效配方。这些发现在13.5B MoE模型大规模训练中得到验证。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解和规模化多模态预训练提供了原则性基础，系统性揭示了模态交互的物理机制，为统一多模态基础模型的设计提供了实证指导。

Abstract: Vision offers a critical axis for advancing foundation models, driving a shift towards natively unified multimodal pretraining. Despite this momentum, the design space and the fundamental mechanisms of how modalities interact during unified training remain underexplored. We provide empirical clarity through a systematic exploration of multimodal pretraining. Our controlled experiments on both synthetic and large-scale real-world datasets yield four key insights into the physics of multimodal pretraining: (i) Knowledge Flow: We disentangle how language, visual understanding, and visual generation transfer knowledge across modalities, revealing distinct patterns of influence and asymmetry; (ii) Synergy vs. Competition: We show that data "complexity" largely determines whether modalities are synergistic, identify architectural choices that promote synergy: such as shared attention and normalization with modality-specific feed-forward layers, and find that these behaviors generalize across different visual tokenizer designs; (iii) Early Unification: Unifying modalities from the very early stages and training them jointly is shown to be more effective than late alignment or sequential training. This process uncovers a vision laziness phenomenon, where delayed integration leads models to rely on language priors; (iv) Recipes: We derive efficient pretraining recipes that achieve strong generative performance using only 5% of the compute budget. These core findings are subsequently validated at scale by training multiple 13.5B MoE models on 2T tokens. We hope this study provides a principled foundation for understanding and scaling multimodal pretraining.

</details>


### [100] [OmniEdit-Bench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Instruction-based Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05049)
*Chenxuan Miao,Yutong Feng,Yi Lu,Yunfeng Yan,Donglian Qi,Shiwei Zhang,Yu Liu,Xi Chen,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了OmniEdit-Bench，一个面向指令式视频编辑（IVE）的全面结构化基准，将编辑任务分解为空间、时间、音频和参考编辑等多维度，并引入了一种基于准确率的惩罚机制来确保评估忠实于指令，弥补了现有基准在任务覆盖和指令遵从性评估上的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IVE评估基准存在两大局限：一是任务覆盖有限，仅继承图像编辑的框架，忽略了视频特有的维度（如时间、音频）；二是评估指标不充分，无法衡量指令忠实度，导致即便编辑错误，基于原始视频的强视觉先验仍可获得高分。

Method: 构建了多维度的IVE基准，涵盖空间、时间、音频和参考式编辑等多种任务类型；区分显式和隐式指令，并引入推理型场景。提出了四维度评估框架（准确性、保真度、真实性、一致性），结合人工评判和先进视觉语言模型，并设计了基于准确率的惩罚机制，使其他评分以准确性为条件，防止视觉上合理但实际错误的编辑获得虚高评分。

Result: 在多种代表性开源和商业模型上进行了大量实验，结果表明当前的IVE模型远未达到令人满意的水平。

Conclusion: OmniEdit-Bench为指令式视频编辑提供了全面可靠的测试平台，并为未来研究方向提供了见解。

Abstract: Instruction-based video editing (IVE) is an emerging field with broad applications, yet evaluating editing models remains challenging. Existing benchmarks suffer from two major limitations: limited task coverage inherited from image editing, which overlooks video-specific dimensions, and inadequate metrics that fail to measure instruction fidelity, allowing incorrect edits to receive high scores due to strong visual priors from the original video. To address these issues, we introduce a comprehensive and structured benchmark for IVE. Our benchmark decomposes editing tasks into multiple video-specific dimensions, including spatial, temporal, audio, and reference-based editing, extending beyond conventional frame-level evaluation. It also distinguishes explicit and implicit instructions and incorporates reasoning-based scenarios to better reflect real-world requirements. Furthermore, we propose an evaluation framework that assesses editing quality from four complementary dimensions: accuracy, preservation, realism, and consistency, using both human judgments and state-of-the-art vision-language models. To emphasize instruction fidelity, we introduce an accuracy-aware penalty mechanism that conditions other scores on accuracy, preventing visually plausible but incorrect edits from receiving inflated evaluations. Extensive experiments on representative open-source and commercial models show that current IVE models remain far from satisfactory. OmniEdit-Bench provides a comprehensive and reliable testbed for evaluating instruction-based video editing and offers insights into future research directions.

</details>


### [101] [OPD-V: Visual On-Policy Self-Distillation with Modality Balance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05131)
*Aniri,Jinhe Bi,Peng Liao,Zengjie Jin,Volker Tresp,Fei Shen,Yunpu Ma,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OPD-V，一种基于模态平衡的视觉自蒸馏范式。通过构造正负教师（分别使用放大图像和掩码图像）来显式实例化模态平衡信息，利用正教师模态平衡对数边际定义模态平衡信任区域，筛选用于自蒸馏的on-policy token，从而解决OPSD中因模态失衡导致特权信息利用不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OPSD方法通过多样输入源提取特权信息来引导自蒸馏，但忽略了多模态大语言模型中固有的模态失衡挑战——当文本信息主导生成时，模型无法充分整合多模态输入，导致精心设计的特权信息利用不充分，限制了OPSD的有效性。

Method: 1) 构造正教师（Zoom-In图像）和负教师（Mask图像），二者呈现不同程度的模态失衡；通过推理正确性和token logits变化验证模态平衡本身可作为特权信息。2) 提出OPD-V范式：利用正教师模态平衡对数边际定义模态平衡信任区域，用于选择用于自蒸馏的on-policy tokens。

Result: 在6个基准、4种MLLM骨干和5种后训练方法上，OPD-V一致地提升推理性能，同时降低训练成本。

Conclusion: 模态平衡本身是一种有效的特权信息，将其实例化到自蒸馏流程中能够在提升多模态推理能力的同时降低训练开销，为OPSD提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) has become a standard post-training approach for improving visual reasoning in multimodal large language models (MLLMs). Existing methods draw privileged information from diverse input sources to guide self-distillation. Yet these designs overlook Modality Imbalance, a challenge inherent to MLLM reasoning. When textual information dominates generation, the model cannot fully integrate its multimodal input. Consequently, carefully designed privileged information remains underused, limiting the effectiveness of OPSD. To examine this limitation, we construct a Positive Teacher with the Zoom-In Image and a Negative Teacher with the Mask Image, which exhibit different degrees of Modality Imbalance. Changes in their reasoning correctness and token logits reveal that Modality Balance can itself serve as privileged information. Motivated by this finding, we introduce OPD-V, a visual OPSD paradigm that instantiates such information through the Positive Teacher and Negative Teacher. Positive Modality-Balance Logits Margins define a Modality-Balance Trust Region that selects the on-policy tokens used for self-distillation. Experiments across 6 benchmarks, 4 MLLM backbones, and 5 post-training methods show that OPD-V consistently improves reasoning performance while reducing training cost.

</details>


### [102] [Beyond Reprojection Error: Camera Calibration with 3D Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05066)
*Dennis Ruppel,Hasan Kutlu,Kai A. Neumann,Martin Knuth,Pedro Santos,Andreas Weinmann,Arjan Kuijper*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向3D重建的相机标定框架，基于场景射线预测而非传统2D平面标定，结合新颖的射线度量（重建误差和相交误差）与自助统计方法，评估不同标定目标与流程，并设计了二十面体标定目标以提升标定精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的相机标定方法主要依赖2D平面标定，但在3D重建场景中，2D标定范式不够灵活，且重投影误差可能无法真实反映3D空间的精度。作者旨在提出一种专门针对3D重建的标定框架，利用场景射线预测提升标定的灵活性和准确性。

Method: 该框架基于预测场景射线进行标定，采用重建误差与相交误差两种新度量替代传统重投影误差；结合bootstrap自助抽样程序，统计评估不同标定目标与标定流程的内外参效果；设计了二十面体标定目标及基于环形特征的检测器以丰富3D重建的标定信息。

Result: 广义畸变模型能更真实地捕捉物理相机效应并提升标定精度；重投影误差被证明可能误导3D精度评估，所提射线度量更全面；二十面体标定目标在合成数据上相比基准方法平均相交误差降低约40%，标定稳定性更强，但真实数据表现对制造公差要求极高。

Conclusion: 射线驱动的标定框架为3D重建提供了更可靠的标定评估方式，二十面体目标是有效的标定方案，但其实际应用受限于高精度制造工艺的要求。整体工作为3D重建相机标定开辟了新思路。

Abstract: In 3D reconstruction, camera calibration is an essential element for achieving high fidelity and accuracy of the reconstructed geometry. While existing approaches rely upon 2D planar calibration, this work proposes a framework tailored for 3D reconstruction that is based on predicting scene rays, which adds flexibility to the reconstruction pipeline and enables the use of recent advances in camera models. Novel metrics, reconstruction and intersection error, derived from predicted scene rays are employed in combination with a bootstrapping procedure that statistically evaluates different calibration objects and calibration pipelines for both intrinsic and extrinsic camera parameters. The results show that the generalized distortion model more faithfully captures physical camera effects and yields an improvement in calibration accuracy. Reprojection error is shown to be a potentially misleading indicator of 3D accuracy, and the proposed ray-based metrics provide a more holistic assessment. An icosahedron calibration target is designed to enrich calibration information for 3D reconstruction together with a ring-feature-based detector. The icosahedral target yields approximately 40% lower mean intersection and more stable calibration across bootstrap trials on synthetic data, while real-data performance demands very tight fabrication tolerances.

</details>


### [103] [HelloWorld: Enabling Socially Interactive Characters in Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05070)
*Liangyang Ouyang,Ruicong Liu,Xuangeng Chu,Kaipeng Zhang,Yoichi Sato*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HelloWorld 是一个支持用户与视频世界模型中角色进行社交互动的视频世界模型。用户只需按一次按钮，即可提示屏幕中的角色面向镜头做出回应（如转身、挥手、点头或说话）。论文提出自蒸馏管道和训练自由的交互时序模块，并构建了 HelloWorldBench 基准来评估交互质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管视频世界模型近期取得了显著进展，但用户与世界中角色之间的社交互动仍不被支持，这是一个尚未填补的空白。论文旨在填充这一缺口，让用户能与生成视频中的角色进行自然的社交交互。

Method: 1) 提出自蒸馏管道：在模型自身合成的数据上微调视频生成模型，每个合成片段同时包含社交互动和相机运动，使模型学习相机姿态条件而不降低交互质量。2) 提出训练自由的"何时交互"模块：在推理时，用户按下按钮后，该模块调制 DiT 的交叉注意力掩码，使交互相关文本提示仅关注按钮按下窗口内的帧，在时间上定位角色的响应。3) 构建 HelloWorldBench：包含400个样本，使用三个社交交互指标和三个传统指标进行评估。

Result: 实验表明，HelloWorld 在交互质量上超越了多种基线方法，同时在图片美学质量和相机姿态跟随方面保持最先进水平。

Conclusion: HelloWorld 成功实现了视频世界模型中用户与角色的社交互动，通过自蒸馏管道和训练自由的注意力调制机制，在不牺牲生成质量和相机控制能力的前提下，实现了高质量的角色交互响应。

Abstract: Despite the remarkable recent progress of video world models, social interaction between users and the characters within these worlds remains unsupported. To fill this gap, we present HelloWorld, a video world model that enables social interaction with in-world characters. With a single button press, users can prompt the on-screen character to respond toward the camera, e.g., turning to the viewer, waving, nodding, or speaking a short greeting. To make these interactions natural, we propose a self-distillation pipeline that finetunes the video generation model on data synthesized by itself. Each synthesized clip contains both social interactions and camera motion, allowing the model to learn camera-pose conditioning without degrading interaction quality. At inference, we further introduce a training-free module that determines when the interaction occurs. Upon a button press, it modulates the cross-attention masks of the DiT so that the interaction-related text prompt attends only to the frames within the press window, temporally localizing the character's response. We further build HelloWorldBench, a 400-sample benchmark with three social interaction metrics alongside three conventional metrics, for evaluation. Experiments demonstrate that HelloWorld surpasses a variety of baselines in interaction quality, while maintaining state-of-the-art picture aesthetics and camera-pose following. Project page: https://github.com/AlayaLab/HelloWorld

</details>


### [104] [Bag-of-Visual-Words for Spatial Mapping of Lung Adenocarcinoma Growth Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05074)
*Darya Ardan,Valentin Oreiller,Henning Müller*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个弱监督的Bag-of-Visual-Words（BoVW）流水线，用于肺腺癌（LUAD）全切片图像（WSI）的空间模式映射。该方法从少量标注的ROI中利用冻结的Foundation model嵌入学习视觉词汇，通过均值BoVW直方图构建模式原型，并在Jensen-Shannon散度下进行最近原型分类，最终将预测投影到WSI瓦片网格上生成可解释的空间模式图。在87例CPTAC-LUAD患者上评估，肿瘤/健康分类达到0.974的平衡准确率，组织学分级分类优于监督基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在单个瓦片层级操作并产生通用形态学聚类，而非临床定义的模式图。肺腺癌生长模式的空间映射需要在区域层级解析结构上下文，因此需要一种能够生成可解释、临床相关的空间模式图的方法。

Method: 采用弱监督BoVW流水线：1）从少量标注ROI中提取冻结的foundation model嵌入；2）学习视觉词汇（visual vocabulary）；3）将同标签ROI的均值BoVW直方图作为模式原型；4）在Jensen-Shannon散度下进行滑动窗口区域的最近原型分类；5）将预测投影到WSI瓦片网格生成空间模式图。评估使用3种foundation model编码器和多种词汇量大小。

Result: 在87例CPTAC-LUAD患者上：肿瘤/健康分类最佳配置（H-Optimus-1）平衡准确率达0.974，接近监督SVM（基于均值池化WSI嵌入）的0.987；二元组织学分级分类中，BoVW流水线在所有编码器下均优于监督基线，表明ROI级模式分解保留了全局均值池化所削弱的等级相关异质性。

Conclusion: BoVW方法能够在区域级别生成临床相关的空间模式图，并保留了对组织学分级分类重要的异质性信息，为LUAD生长模式的空间映射提供了一种弱监督且可解释的替代方案。

Abstract: Spatial mapping of lung adenocarcinoma (LUAD) growth patterns across whole slide images (WSIs) requires resolving architectural context at the region level, yet existing methods operate at the individual tile level and produce generic morphological clusters rather than clinically defined pattern maps. We propose a weakly supervised Bag-of-Visual-Words (BoVW) pipeline that learns a visual vocabulary from frozen foundation model embeddings extracted from a small set of annotated regions of interest (ROIs). Pattern prototypes are constructed as mean BoVW histograms of same-label ROIs and used for nearest-prototype classification of sliding-window regions under Jensen--Shannon divergence. The resulting predictions are projected onto the WSI tile grid to produce interpretable spatial pattern maps. We evaluate the method on 87 CPTAC-LUAD patients using three foundation model encoders and multiple vocabulary sizes on two clinically motivated tasks. For tumour/healthy classification, the best configuration achieves a balanced accuracy of $0.974$ with H-Optimus-1, approaching the $0.987$ obtained by a supervised SVM trained on mean-pooled WSI embeddings. For binary histologic grade classification, the BoVW pipeline achieves higher balanced accuracy than the supervised baseline for all encoders, suggesting that ROI-level pattern decomposition preserves grade-relevant heterogeneity that is attenuated by global mean pooling.

</details>


### [105] [Lesion Detection in CT with Frozen Self-Distilled Features: SALT, a Spatially Adaptive Label-Guided Temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05100)
*Mahmut S. Gokmen,Evan W. Damron,Mitchell A. Klusty,Caroline N. Leach,Emily B. Collier,V. K. Cody Bumgardner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SALT（Spatially Adaptive Label-guided Temperature），一种空间自适应的自蒸馏目标调节方法。通过在自监督预训练期间利用弱框标注，对病灶区域内的教师softmax温度进行锐化并上调掩码patch损失权重，从而让病灶在训练信号中获得更大权重，而不改变模型看到的内容。在四个CT队列的3D病灶检测任务中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自监督预训练目标在空间上是均匀的——教师温度和逐patch损失权重在整个图像中完全相同，导致小块病灶对训练信号的贡献不高于周围正常实质。先前的工作只是将视图偏向已标注区域，改变模型看到的内容但未对目标函数施加压力。

Method: SALT方法在自蒸馏过程中条件化目标：利用预训练阶段才可用的弱框标注，在编码器的patch网格上定义一个紧凑区域，在该区域内锐化教师的softmax温度并上调掩码patch损失的权重。目标函数、掩码策略和centering统计量均保持不变，下游使用时编码器作为无标签无条件的普通特征提取器。

Result: 冻结编码器并仅训练轻量级多深度CenterNet风格头部，在四个CT队列上检测3D病灶。报告了patch级可分性、按队列和病灶大小分层的3D检测结果、框质量，以及无检测器的探针实验——单个冻结patch嵌入可在无配准、无掩码、无微调的情况下在随访扫描中重新识别病灶。与架构、预训练数据、调度和标签引导裁剪相同但无目标条件化的基线骨干隔离验证了机制的有效性。

Conclusion: 由于条件化通过空间指示器而非标签语义表达，该公式适用于任何弱空间标注形式。方法已被实例化并验证用于病灶检测场景。

Abstract: Self-supervised pretraining objectives are spatially uniform: the teacher temperature and the per-patch loss weight are identical everywhere in the image, so a lesion a few patches wide contributes no more to the training signal than the surrounding parenchyma. Prior work biases the views toward annotated regions, which changes what the model sees but adds no pressure on the objective. We instead condition the targets of self-distillation, a method we call SALT (Spatially Adaptive Label-guided Temperature). Weak, box-derived labels, available only during pretraining, define a compact region on the encoder's patch grid, inside which the teacher's softmax temperature is sharpened and the masked-patch loss is up-weighted. The objectives, the masking policy and the centering statistics are otherwise unchanged, and at every downstream use the encoder is a plain feature extractor with no labels and no conditioning. We evaluate by freezing the encoder and training only a lightweight multi-depth CenterNet-style head, detecting lesions in 3D on four CT cohorts, and we isolate the mechanism against a backbone identical in architecture, pretraining data, schedule and label-guided cropping but with no target conditioning. We report patch-level separability, 3D detection stratified by cohort and by lesion size, box quality, and a detector-free probe in which a single frozen patch embedding re-identifies a lesion in a follow-up scan without registration, masks or fine-tuning. Because the conditioning is expressed through a spatial indicator rather than through label semantics, the formulation admits any weak spatial annotation; we instantiate and validate it for lesions.

</details>


### [106] [HexMIL: Hierarchical Attention MIL for Ante-Hoc Explainable Detection of AI-Manipulated CT Volumes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05101)
*Orazio Pontorno,Luca Guarnera,Zahid Akhtar,Sebastiano Battiato*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HexMIL是一种基于层级多示例学习（Hierarchical Explainable Multiple Instance Learning）的无掩码医学深度伪造检测器，仅利用二进制体级标签即可同时实现检测与可解释定位，通过将CT体分解为patch和slice两级层次结构，并使用独立门控注意力模块聚合，其注意力权重可直接构成全分辨率3D注意力图，在跨生成器泛化协议下优于所有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学深度伪造（由深度生成模型操纵的医学图像）对临床工作流构成严重威胁，但现有检测器存在两个关键局限：对未见过的生成架构泛化能力差，且缺乏可解释性。这两个问题亟需同时解决。

Method: HexMIL将每个CT体分解为patch和slice组成的两级层次结构，通过相互独立的门控注意力模块进行聚合。其注意力权重可直接组合成全分辨率3D注意力体，用于定位被操纵的子区域，无需任何像素级标注。与Grad-CAM等事后解释方法不同，HexMIL的注意力权重构成驱动分类决策的前向计算过程本身，提供事前（ante-hoc）且结构上忠实的空间归因。

Result: 在M3DSynth和CT-GAN数据集上，采用严格的跨生成器泛化协议（在单一生成架构上训练、在未见架构上测试），HexMIL在域外分类中相比所有基线在AUC上提升+9.1、F1上提升+9.4，并在定位任务中取得最佳平均IoU和Pointing Game分数。

Conclusion: HexMIL同时解决了医学深度伪造检测中泛化能力差和缺乏可解释性的核心问题，仅依靠二进制体级监督即可实现高性能的检测与像素级定位，是一种结构上忠实的ante-hoc可解释检测框架。

Abstract: The emergence of medical deepfakes, i.e., medical images manipulated by deep generative models, poses a significant threat to clinical workflows. However, existing detectors suffer from two critical limitations: poor generalization to unseen generative architectures for manipulation detection and lack of interpretability. In this context, we present HexMIL (Hierarchical EXplainable Multiple Instance Learning), a mask-free medical deepfake detector that simultaneously addresses both limitations using only binary volume-level supervision. HexMIL decomposes each CT volume into a two-level hierarchy of patches and slices, aggregated via independent Gated Attention modules whose weights are directly combined into a full-resolution 3D attention volume that localizes the manipulated sub-region without any pixel-level annotation. Unlike post-hoc methods such as Grad-CAM, HexMIL's attention weights constitute the exact forward computation driving the classification decision, providing ante-hoc and structurally faithful spatial attribution. We evaluate HexMIL on M3DSynth and CT-GAN datasets under a rigorous cross-generator generalization protocol, training on a single generative architecture and testing on unseen ones. HexMIL outperforms all baselines by $+9.1$ AUC and $+9.4$ F1 in out-of-domain classification, and achieves the best average IoU and Pointing Game score in localization. Project page: opontorno.github.io/hexmil.

</details>


### [107] [IRIS: A Visual Cortex-Inspired Framework for Analyzing Orientation Selectivity in Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05122)
*Vaishnavi B Mohan,Vijayakrishna Naganoor,Yashas Annadani,Shashank Hegde*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了视觉Transformer（ViT）中方向选择性（orientation selectivity）这一生物启发的低层特征是如何形成的。作者引入了一组神经科学启发的度量指标（RSS、ORS和方向调谐带宽），发现训练范式是方向选择性最强的决定因素，早期到中层单元在训练中逐渐获得方向选择性，而深层单元则丧失选择性转向语义编码。这些度量还可用于指导迁移学习中的层解冻策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ViT已成为视觉编码的事实标准，但其缺乏归纳偏置（如局部感受野），因此机制上尚不清楚它们如何编码低层级特征。相比之下，生物视觉系统（如初级视觉皮层V1）通过结合视野中的小局部区域来构建方向选择性等低层特征，这些特征是通用表征，被多个专用神经通路共享。作者希望探究这种生物基础特征是否在ViT中出现以及如何出现。

Method: 作者引入了一套神经科学启发的度量指标：(1) 表征相似性分数（RSS）用于衡量方向在表征几何中的编码方式；(2) 方向招募分数（ORS）用于量化定向单元的数量；(3) 方向调谐带宽。通过这些指标，作者系统分析了ViT中方向选择性随模型深度和训练过程的变化。

Result: 主要发现包括：(1) 训练范式是方向选择性的最强决定因素，共享相同目标的模型在相对深度上达到峰值，与模型规模无关；(2) 许多单元在训练早期就具有方向选择性，早期到中间层在训练中会招募更多此类单元，而深层单元则失去选择性并拓宽调谐转向语义编码；(3) 这些度量提供了一种机制性启发式方法，用于判断迁移学习中应解冻多少层以获得最佳下游泛化性能。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种在ViT训练过程中追踪生物基础特征的方法，探究了期望属性如何在Transformer表征中被编码，并建立了对ViT跨任务泛化能力的系统性理解。

Abstract: Vision transformers (ViTs) have become the de facto standard for image encoding across many perception tasks. Despite their empirical success, it remains mechanistically unclear how they encode low-level features, given their lack of inductive biases: ViTs process information globally rather than relying on local structure. Biological visual systems, in contrast, build low-level features, such as orientation selectivity in the primary visual cortex, by combining information from small, localized regions of the visual field. These features are general-purpose representations, shared and required across multiple specialized neural pathways, unlike higher-level, task-specific semantic features. This raises the question if such biologically-grounded features arise in ViTs. In this work, we systematically study how orientation selectivity emerges in ViTs by introducing a suite of neuroscience-inspired metrics: representational similarity score (RSS), orientation recruitment score (ORS), and orientation tuning bandwidth to quantify how orientation is encoded in representational geometry and as a function of model depth. Through extensive analysis, we find that: (1) the training paradigm is the strongest determinant of orientation selectivity, with models sharing an objective, peaking at comparable relative depths regardless of scale (2) many units are orientation-selective early in training, with early-to-middle layers recruiting more such units over time, while deeper layers lose selectivity and broaden their tuning toward semantic encoding and (3) our metrics offer a mechanistic heuristic for how many layers to unfreeze for best downstream generalization. Our framework presents a way to track biologically-grounded features during ViT training, probes how desired properties are encoded in transformer representations, and builds a systematic understanding of how ViTs generalize across tasks.

</details>


### [108] [Predicting Brain Morphometry with MT-GNN: Mesh Evolution in Continuous Time with Graph-Based Metric Tensor Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05132)
*Hao Ding,Daniel Semchin,Paul M. Thompson,Boris Gutman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MT-GNN，一种基于图神经网络的亚皮层结构形状演变预测模型，通过预测连续时间内的表面内在几何（度量张量），并使用可微分的ARAP求解器解码为有效表面，实现端到端训练。在ADNI数据集的14个亚皮层结构上，该方法在所有预测时域上均优于现有方法（平均顶点误差较时间均值低2.29%，p=6.1×10⁻⁵，14/14结构均胜出），且优势随预测时域增大而扩大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从少量先验扫描预测亚皮层结构形状演变，可支持预后判断和临床试验富集。现有方法（如高维嵌入外推形状轨迹或直接回归顶点形变）存在局限，需要更精准的预测方法。

Method: 提出MT-GNN：单个每结构图网络在连续时间内预测表面内在几何——每个顶点的第一基本形式（度量张量），可处理任意因果多次访问历史和任意预测时域，以lead time的傅里叶编码为条件。预测的度量通过可微分的ARAP（As-Rigid-As-Possible）求解器解码为表面，模型在刚性对齐顶点误差上端到端训练。

Result: 在ADNI数据集14个亚皮层结构上，MT-GNN在所有时域预测中表现最佳：平均顶点误差较时间均值降低2.29%（p=6.1×10⁻⁵），在14/14个结构上均优于基线；显著超过测地线形状回归DCM（-0.19%）和网格Transformer TransforMesh（-0.45%，p=1.2×10⁻⁴），且优势随预测时域增大而扩大。通过重建路径训练始终能提升解码预测的有效性和精度。

Conclusion: 通过预测表面内在几何并在可微重建过程中端到端训练，MT-GNN能够生成有效的形状演变预测且在准确性上显著优于现有方法，为亚皮层结构长期演变的预测提供了更可靠的技术方案。

Abstract: Predicting how a subcortical structure's shape will evolve from a few prior scans could support prognosis and clinical-trial enrichment. Existing longitudinal mesh predictors either extrapolate shape trajectories via high-dimensional embeddings or regress vertex deformations directly. We instead predict the surface's intrinsic geometry in continuous time: a single per-structure graph network predicts the future per-vertex first fundamental form (metric tensor) for an arbitrary causal multiple-visit history and an arbitrary prediction horizon, conditioned on a Fourier encoding of the lead time. The predicted metric is decoded into a surface by a differentiable As-Rigid-As-Possible solver, and the model is trained end-to-end on the rigid-aligned vertex error. Training through the reconstruction keeps the decoded prediction a valid surface and consistently improves it. On 14 subcortical structures from the ADNI dataset, the proposed mesh evolution model (MT-GNN) predicts best among the evaluated methods at every horizon ($-2.29\%$ mean vertex error vs. the temporal mean, $p{=}6.1{\times}10^{-5}$, beating it on 14/14 structures), ahead of geodesic shape regression (DCM, $-0.19\%$) and a mesh transformer (TransforMesh, $-0.45\%$; $p{=}1.2{\times}10^{-4}$), with the lead widening as the horizon grows.

</details>


### [109] [SmartMage: Dynamic Modality Orchestration for 3D Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05137)
*Yue Zhang,Yingzhao Jian,Yunqiu Xu,Xiaoxiao Sun,Hehe Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SmartMage，一种统一的MLLM，通过语义感知的方式动态编排异构模态（视觉与几何）用于3D场景理解，克服现有模型固定模态组合导致的计算浪费与推理稀释问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在处理3D场景时通常依赖固定的模态组合，忽略了不同查询对模态的需求差异。这种刚性设计会引入无关模态的语义噪声，同时未能充分利用更有信息量的模态，导致计算浪费和推理效果稀释。

Method: SmartMage包含两个核心模块：(1) SMART（Semantic-guided Modality Adaptive Routing）模块，利用语义先验、文本-模态对齐和模态质量来动态选择任务相关的模态；(2) MAGE（Modality-Aware Gating Expert）模块，利用模态先验引导专家激活，促进多模态推理中的自适应专业化。

Result: SmartMage在5个3D场景理解基准上达到最先进的性能，并在纯RGB视频理解基准上取得有竞争力的结果。在诊断基准ScanFacet中，任务被细分为细粒度语义类别，用于分析每种语义类型偏好的模态组合，观察到的模态-语义模式进一步验证了SmartMage的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过动态模态编排，SmartMage根据查询语义自适应选择最相关的模态，既避免无关模态的干扰又能充分利用信息丰富的模态，从而在3D场景理解任务上取得显著提升，并揭示了模态与语义类型之间的可解释关联模式。

Abstract: Understanding 3D scenes is fundamental to embodied intelligence, requiring joint reasoning over heterogeneous information from multiple modalities, including visual and geometric cues. However, the relevance of these modalities often varies across queries. Existing Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) typically rely on fixed modality combinations, overlooking query-dependent modality needs. Such a rigid design can introduce semantic noise from irrelevant modalities while underutilizing more informative ones, leading to wasted computation and diluted reasoning. To address these challenges, this paper proposes SmartMage, a unified MLLM that dynamically orchestrates heterogeneous modalities for semantic-aware 3D scene understanding. Specifically, SmartMage incorporates: (1) a Semantic-guided Modality Adaptive RouTng (SMART) module that selects task-relevant modalities using semantic priors, text-modality alignment, and modality quality; and (2) a Modality-Aware Gating Expert (MAGE) module that leverages modality priors to guide expert activation, fostering adaptive specialization in multimodal reasoning. Empirically, SmartMage achieves state-of-the-art performance across five 3D scene understanding benchmarks, and attains competitive results on RGB-only video understanding benchmarks. In our diagnostic benchmark ScanFacet, tasks are divided into fine-grained semantic categories, enabling analysis of modality combinations preferred by each semantic type. The observed modality-semantic patterns provide further evidence of SmartMage's effectiveness. Project page: https://yuecheong.github.io/SmartMage/.

</details>


### [110] [Objects as Audio-Visual Modal Sound Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05145)
*Zisen Shao,Zihao Wei,Derong Jin,Ruohan Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AV-MSF，一种新的物体级声学表征方法，通过多视角图像和少量撞击声录音重建物体撞击声场。它将3D高斯泼溅与稠密3D视觉特征结合，提供几何感知先验，并用紧凑的物理模态参数表示撞击声场，实现少样本重建。在两个真实数据集上达到SOTA效果，并支持接触定位和物体声音编辑等下游应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代3D重建擅长建模物体几何与外观，但忽略了物理交互中丰富的声学线索。物体撞击声蕴含材质、刚度与结构属性，可补充视觉信息。然而现有撞击声建模方法要么依赖昂贵的物理仿真，要么需要大规模数据支持纯数据驱动的泛化，缺乏高效且物理可解释的方案。

Method: AV-MSF基于3D高斯泼溅框架，集成稠密3D视觉特征以提供强几何感知先验；同时采用紧凑且物理意义明确的模态参数来表示撞击声场，从而实现鲁棒的少样本重建。整个方法只需多视角图像和少量撞击声录音作为输入。

Result: 在两个真实世界数据集上的实验表明，AV-MSF在撞击声渲染任务上达到最先进水平，优于基于物理仿真和数据驱动的基线方法。此外，该表征还支持接触定位和物体声音编辑等下游应用。

Conclusion: AV-MSF验证了将视觉几何先验与物理模态参数相结合的声场表示方法的有效性，在少样本条件下即可实现高质量的撞击声渲染，并具备良好可扩展性以支持多种下游任务，弥补了传统3D重建中声学信息的缺失。

Abstract: While modern 3D reconstruction excels at modeling object geometry and appearance, it largely ignores the rich acoustic cues revealed through physical interaction. Object impact sounds convey material, stiffness, and structural properties that complement vision, yet existing impact sound modeling approaches either rely on expensive physics-based simulation or require large datasets to generalize in a purely data-driven manner. We introduce Audio-Visual Modal Sound Field (AV-MSF), a novel object-level acoustic representation reconstructed from multi-view images and only a few impact sound recordings. AV-MSF builds on 3D Gaussian Splatting integrated with dense 3D visual feature to provide a strong geometry-aware prior, and represents the impact sound field using compact, physically meaningful modal parameters, enabling robust few-shot reconstruction. Experiments on two real-world datasets show that AV-MSF achieves state-of-the-art impact sound rendering, outperforming both physics-based and data-driven baselines. Furthermore, we demonstrate downstream applications enabled by our representation, including contact localization and object sound editing.

</details>


### [111] [CoCo-IR: Contextual Composed Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05149)
*Shengcao Cao,Tanmaya Shekhar Dabral,Zhongli Ding,Madhuri Shanbhogue,Kaifeng Chen,Zhe Li,Mojtaba Seyedhosseini,Yu-Xiong Wang,Liang-Yan Gui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了上下文组合图像检索（CoCo-IR）新任务，使指令式图像检索系统能从单轮交互扩展为多轮渐进式检索。作者基于大型多模态模型（LMM）构建了一个上下文感知推理器，生成可跨轮次演化的可变换图像嵌入（TIE）；并设计了全自动可扩展的数据引擎，借助LMM生成高质量上下文检索数据及模型引导的困难负样本挖掘。实验表明该方法在单轮基准CIRCO上达到39.4 mAP@5的新SOTA，在CoCo-IR基准上4轮对话达到44.1 R@1，远超现有方法（28.2 R@1）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于指令的图像检索系统仅支持单轮交互，无法捕捉真实复杂视觉搜索中用户逐步细化查询需求的迭代特性，亟需一种能够处理多轮上下文的图像检索范式。

Method: 提出CoCo-IR任务，设计了基于LMM的上下文感知推理器模型，通过理解完整交互历史生成随轮次演进的可变换图像嵌入（TIE）；同时开发了无需人工标注的全自动可扩展数据引擎，利用LMM生成高质量检索数据，并通过模型引导验证挖掘困难负样本以增强训练效果。

Result: 在单轮基准CIRCO上取得39.4 mAP@5的新SOTA成绩；在新构建的CoCo-IR基准上，4轮对话取得44.1 R@1，显著优于现有方法（28.2 R@1），证明模型在多轮上下文检索中的鲁棒性和优越性。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入多轮上下文检索任务CoCo-IR及配套的LMM架构和数据生成管线，有效解决了单轮检索系统无法应对迭代式视觉搜索的局限，为指令式图像检索向多轮交互方向的发展提供了新的范式与强基线。

Abstract: Current instruction-based image retrieval systems are powerful but limited to single-turn interactions, failing to capture the iterative nature of complex, real-world visual searches. To overcome this limitation, we introduce Contextual Composed Image Retrieval (CoCo-IR), a novel task that enables users to progressively refine search results through interactions. We address this new task by proposing a new model based on a Large Multimodal Model (LMM) that functions as a context-aware reasoner for CoCo-IR. Our model interprets the entire interaction history to generate Transformable Image Embeddings (TIE) that evolve across turns. To fuel the model training without expensive human annotations, we develop a fully autonomous, scalable data engine that leverages LMMs to generate high-quality contextual retrieval data, and uses model-guided verification to mine challenging hard negatives. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach establishes new state-of-the-art performance: We achieve 39.4 mAP@5 on the challenging single-turn benchmark CIRCO; furthermore, on our new CoCo-IR benchmark, our model maintains robust performance with 44.1 R@1 on 4-turn dialogues, dramatically outperforming existing methods (28.2 4-turn R@1) that fail to handle multi-turn context. Project page: https://CoCo-IR.github.io.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [112] [A Long-Run Persistence Theory for AI Systems under the Redundancy-Adjusted Artificial Age Score (AAS)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04012)
*Seyma Yaman Kayadibi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于冗余调整人工年龄得分（AAS）的长期持久性框架，将AAS从静态评估指标扩展为周期级泛函，证明AI系统在无限循环运行下的结构年龄可以保持有界，而非必然累积恶化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统越来越多地需要在重复的交互、适应和更新周期中运行，而非一次性输出。这引出一个根本性理论问题：AI系统能否无限期持续运行而不产生无界的结构老化？现有AAS仅作为静态评估指标，缺乏对长期运行过程的动态分析框架。

Method: 将AAS从静态评估指标扩展为周期级泛函，生成跨重复操作的结构年龄序列。在每个周期，通过加权、冗余感知的对数惩罚函数，基于组件一致性水平定义结构年龄。在此框架下建立了渐近机制层次（负担持久性、零负担持久性、振荡持久性和累积终端负担），并确立比较排序、敏感性界、组件稳定化下的收敛性、有限全变差下的持久性、阻尼周期间扰动下的几何稳定化以及非退化冗余条件下的零负担刻画。

Result: 主要结果是：无限循环延续不需要无界结构老化。AI系统可以经历无限多个周期而其结构年龄保持有界；在更强正则条件下其边际老化消失；在最强机制下其周期级负担收敛为零。

Conclusion: 该框架为分析长期人工智能持久性提供了形式化基础，将其视为有界结构负担问题而非不可避免的累积恶化问题。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence systems are increasingly expected to operate over repeated cycles of interaction, adaptation, and update rather than through isolated one-shot outputs. This raises a fundamental theoretical question: can an AI system persist indefinitely without incurring unbounded structural aging? This paper develops a long-run persistence framework for AI systems based on the redundancy-adjusted Artificial Age Score (AAS). The model extends AAS from a static evaluative measure into a cycle-level functional that generates an age sequence across repeated operation. At each cycle, structural age is defined through a weighted, redundancy-aware logarithmic penalty over component consistency levels. Within this framework, cycle-level age is shown to be well defined and uniformly bounded, thereby excluding explosive pointwise aging. On this basis, the paper defines a hierarchy of asymptotic regimes, including burdened persistence, zero-burden persistence, oscillatory persistence, and cumulative terminal burden. It also establishes comparative ordering, sensitivity bounds, convergence under componentwise stabilization, persistence under finite total variation, geometric stabilization under damped inter-cycle perturbations, and a zero-burden characterization under nondegenerate redundancy conditions. The main result is that indefinite cyclic continuation does not require unbounded structural aging: an AI system may pass through infinitely many cycles while its structural age remains bounded, while under stronger regularity conditions its marginal aging vanishes and, in the strongest regime, its cycle-level burden converges to zero. The framework thus provides a formal basis for analyzing long-run artificial persistence as a problem of bounded structural burden rather than inevitable cumulative deterioration.

</details>


### [113] [The LLM Proposes, the Executive Disposes: A Self-Verifying Agent Instrument that Dissociates Commitment Drift from Binding Drift in Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04066)
*Mohsen Arjmandi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结构化验证的Agent仪器：确定性执行者拥有全部信念，语言模型仅能提交类型化提案，预言在行动前注册、由代码按观察匹配。该仪器通过自我失效机制和渲染不可见的影子参考来实现结构性验证，并在ARC-AGI-3任务上报告了零级完成率的全披露结果。

验证是结构性的而非事后性的：失败由代码检测，每轮运行可自我失效。该方法使漂移指标在机制被测除的消融单元中也具有定义，从而实现了漂移分解测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证长时程Agent的根本困难在于：Agent自身的状态和自我报告恰恰是不可信任的。传统的事后验证方式无法解决这一信任问题，因此需要一种结构性的验证系统，使得验证是内建于架构而非附加于事后分析的。

Method: 核心设计包括三个支柱：（1）一个确定性的执行者（Executive）拥有全部信念，语言模型只能提交类型化提案；（2）声明仅在行动前预注册的预测被代码与观察匹配后才被采纳；（3）每个运行单元可在写错误、渲染大小或盐化金丝雀回声下限被突破时自我失效。此外，通过渲染不可见的影子参考编译完整系统在每次消融单元中本会承诺的计划，使漂移指标在机制被移除后仍可定义。

Result: 四项关键结果：（1）前8次架构运行中有4次被失效，每次定位到真实缺陷；（2）消融承诺机制使目标抛弃率从0.00翻转为1.00，而绑定错误保持平坦0.00（每个单元3个种子，每次运行最多394个参考节拍，所有运行有效）；（3）绑定通道的修复被消融时，每节拍漂移未再现——因为绑定由代码拥有，失败类别被结构性吸收，其残余仅在上游一层以假设形成崩溃的形式出现；（4）任务效能为零（52次有效运行在ARC-AGI-3上零级完成），被预先注册为结构败因。

Conclusion: 本研究的贡献是提供了一种用于Agent开发的验证方法论以及其可量化的漂移分解。尽管任务效能为零，该零结果本身是具有信息量的预注册结构性败因，展示了在任务失败的情况下仍可进行严格验证的可能性。

Abstract: How do you verify a long-horizon agent when its own state and self-reports are exactly what you cannot trust? We present an agent instrument built so that verification is structural rather than post-hoc. A deterministic Executive owns all belief; a language model may only file typed proposals, and a claim is admitted only when a prediction pre-registered before acting is matched against observation by code. Two properties make the instrument a verifier of its own science, not just of the agent: every run invalidates itself when per-organ write-error, render-size, or salted-canary-echo floors are breached (four of the first eight architecture runs were invalidated, each localizing a real defect); and a render-invisible shadow reference compiles the plan the full system would have committed in every ablation cell, so drift metrics are defined even where the mechanism under test has been removed. Using this instrument we report a clean, single-variable result on a failure every long-horizon agent suffers: ablating the commitment mechanism flips goal-abandonment from 0.00 to 1.00 while binding error stays flat at 0.00 (three seeds per cell, up to 394 reference beats per run, every run gated valid). The binding channel, by contrast, does not reappear as per-beat drift when its repair is ablated -- because binding is code-owned, the failure class is structurally absorbed, its only residue appearing one layer upstream as a collapse in hypothesis formation. We report these under full disclosure that task efficacy is null (zero level completions across 52 gated runs on ARC-AGI-3), pre-registered as a structural defeater. The contribution is a verification methodology for agent development and the drift decomposition it makes measurable.

</details>


### [114] [Monte Carlo Tree Search for Table-to-Multimodal Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04071)
*Teng Lin,Zhiyang Zhang,Yuyu Luo,Nan Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MCTS-Report，一种基于蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）驱动的框架，将多模态表格到报告生成（文本+图表）建模为结构化搜索空间上的渐进式构建过程。该框架将报告生成分解为章节规划、可视化任务识别、图表生成、洞察组织和叙事细化等原子动作，由LLM基于当前报告状态动态推理执行，并通过多维度奖励函数（数值事实一致性、图表质量、图文对齐、结构完整性）引导搜索，配合多样性惩罚和前置条件检查。作者还构建了包含6个领域真实表格及相关参考报告的MMRBench基准，实验表明MCTS-Report在结构完整性、数值准确性、图文对齐和洞察新颖性等指标上显著优于强基线，综合得分77.9。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格到报告生成方法受限于固定的线性流水线和孤立的子任务处理，无法实现事实准确性、视觉质量和叙事连贯性的联合优化，亟需一种能够协同优化这些目标的统一框架。

Method: 将多模态报告生成视为结构化搜索空间上的渐进式构建过程，将生成分解为五项原子动作（章节规划、可视化任务识别、图表生成、洞察组织、叙事细化），每项由LLM基于当前报告状态动态推理执行；采用MCTS进行搜索驱动，LLM生成逐步推理与动作并存储推理轨迹于节点中实现上下文感知生成；设计多维度奖励函数联合评估数值事实一致性（通过SQL验证）、图表质量、图文对齐和结构完整性，并引入多样性惩罚和前置条件检查机制。

Result: 在自建MMRBench基准（覆盖6个领域）上的实验表明，MCTS-Report在结构完整性、数值准确性、图文对齐和洞察新颖性方面显著优于强基线，整体得分为77.9。

Conclusion: MCTS-Report通过将生成过程形式化为可搜索的渐进式构建，成功实现了多模态报告生成中多目标（事实性、视觉质量、叙事连贯性）的联合优化，证明了MCTS驱动的方法在该任务上的有效性。

Abstract: Automatically generating professional multimodal reports comprising both textual analysis and visual charts from structured tabular data is a critical challenge in data intelligence. Existing methods suffer from fixed linear pipelines and isolated subtask processing, which hinder joint optimization of factual accuracy, visual quality, and narrative coherence. To address these issues, this paper proposes MCTS-Report, a Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS)-driven framework that formulates multimodal table-to-report generation as a progressive construction process over a structured search space. The core idea is to decompose report generation into atomic actions, including chapter planning, visualization task identification, chart generation, insight organization, and narrative refinement, each executed by an LLM based on dynamic reasoning conditioned on the current report state. We use an LLM to generate step-by-step reasoning and actions during MCTS, storing the reasoning trajectory in each node for context-aware, coherent report construction. To guide the search, we design a multi-dimensional reward function that jointly evaluates numerical fact consistency (via SQL), chart quality, chart-text alignment, and structural completeness, while incorporating a diversity penalty to suppress repeated charts and a precondition check to prune invalid actions. We also construct MMRBench, a comprehensive benchmark comprising real-world tables from six domains, paired with expert-refined reference report structures and verifiable key insights. Experiments on MMRBench demonstrate that MCTS-Report significantly outperforms strong baselines across structural completeness, numerical accuracy, chart-text alignment, and insight novelty, achieving a 77.9 overall score.

</details>


### [115] [FinProBench: Evaluating Financial AI Agents with Role-Grounded Rubrics Derived from Professional Deliverables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04077)
*Ben Wang,Kang Zhou,Lifan Guo,Feng Chen,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FinProBench基准和Role-Grounded Rubric Construction (RGRC)管道，用于评估金融AI代理。RGRC从实践者交付物中提取隐性标准构建评分标准。实验表明，对于角色专业化任务，RGRC显著优于纯Prompt方法（99.1% vs 78.0%），且角色级标准复用可降低6.7倍构建成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融AI代理评估的评分标准方法通常从任务提示或模型输出中衍生，忽略了只有实践者交付物中才能体现的隐性专业标准（tacit standards），导致评估标准与实际专业工作脱节。

Method: 提出FinProBench基准和RGRC管道。RGRC包含四个阶段：交付物收集（Deliverable Collection）、能力提取（Competency Extraction）、标准综合（Rubric Synthesis）和验证（Validation）。将57个职业按交付物类型分为30个先验丰富常规角色和27个先验稀疏专业角色。构建了覆盖57个职业、8个金融子行业、161种交付物类型的1,723个精选交付物，并发布包含20个完整任务的初始评估集。评估使用异构LLM法官和角色级标准。

Result: 对常规角色，Prompt-only与RGRC表现接近（89.2% vs 90.7%）；但对角色专业化角色，RGRC大幅优于Prompt-only（99.1% vs 78.0%）。人类交付物平均排名第一（73.7分），四个系统分数（70.3、70.2、69.6）均在95%置信区间内重叠。角色级标准复用使单任务构建成本降低6.7倍。

Conclusion: 当惯例在模型先验中良好表征时，提示工程可近似评分标准；但超出模型先验的专业标准必须依赖专业背景支撑（professional grounding）。RGRC能够捕捉隐性标准、区分质量层次并跨任务迁移，是评估金融AI代理的有效方法。

Abstract: Evaluating financial AI agents requires criteria aligned with real professional work. Existing rubric methods typically derive criteria from task prompts or model outputs, overlooking tacit standards visible only in practitioner deliverables. We introduce FinProBench, a benchmark for professional financial tasks, and Role-Grounded Rubric Construction (RGRC), a reusable pipeline that derives rubrics from deliverables produced by practitioners in the same role. RGRC comprises four stages: Deliverable Collection, Competency Extraction, Rubric Synthesis, and Validation. Its rubrics capture tacit standards, distinguish quality levels, and transfer across tasks within a role. Before analysis, we classified 57 occupations by deliverable genre into 30 prior-rich conventional roles and 27 prior-sparse role-specialized roles. Across all roles, Prompt-only nearly matches RGRC for conventional roles (89.2% vs. 90.7%), but RGRC substantially outperforms it for role-specialized roles (99.1% vs. 78.0%). This split indicates that prompt engineering can approximate rubrics when conventions are well represented in model priors, while professional grounding is essential for standards beyond those priors. FinProBench is built from 1,723 curated deliverables spanning 57 occupations, 8 financial sub-industries, and 161 deliverable types, and releases an initial evaluation set of 20 complete tasks covering 20 roles in 7 sub-industries. With heterogeneous LLM judges and role-level rubrics, human deliverables rank first on average (73.7 vs. 70.3, 70.2, and 69.6 out of 100), while all four systems show overlapping 95% confidence intervals and complementary strengths. Reusing rubrics at the role level reduces estimated per-task construction effort by 6.7 times relative to authoring each rubric from scratch.

</details>


### [116] [FinPerMA: A Theory-Informed, Event-Grounded Personalized-Memory Benchmark for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04095)
*Ben Wang,Kang Zhou,Lifan Guo,Feng Chen,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FinPerMA，一个基于事件驱动的个性化记忆基准测试，用于评估LLM智能体在长期纵向投资者轨迹中对用户模型的维护与更新能力。实验发现七个前沿LLM和多种记忆配置均远未饱和，且摘要式记忆常丢失偏好信号，简单检索反而优于专用记忆系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体越来越多地用于金融咨询等高风险的个性化助手场景，但现有个性化记忆基准主要测试事实记忆保留或依赖约束较弱的模型生成轨迹，对事件驱动的偏好适应（即智能体能否根据新事件持续更新用户模型）缺乏充分探索。

Method: 构建FinPerMA基准，其生成流程结合：(1)确定性的、基于理论的影响力规则；(2)受控的LLM叙述生成；(3)自动化质量筛选。同时设置Post-Shock检查点以隔离智能体是否已将重大事件整合进其持久用户模型。数据集包含2,994个问题、276个人格画像，覆盖七个前沿LLM和最多七种记忆配置。

Result: 所有测试配置均远未饱和：全上下文配置的整体准确率不超过约0.47，选择题准确率不超过约39%。归因分析显示摘要式记忆常能保留事实细节却丢失个性化所需的偏好信号，因此简单检索策略反而优于专门构建的记忆系统，且该差距在事件冲击后进一步扩大。

Conclusion: 现有LLM智能体在事件驱动的个性化记忆更新方面仍存在显著不足，偏好信号的保留比事实信息的保留更具挑战性；该发现对高价值场景中个性化助手的设计具有重要启示。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly used as personalized assistants in high-stakes domains such as financial advising, yet it remains unclear whether they can maintain and update an individualized user model over long horizons. Existing personalized-memory benchmarks primarily test factual retention or rely on weakly constrained model-generated trajectories, leaving event-driven preference adaptation underexplored. We introduce FinPerMA, an event-grounded benchmark that evaluates personalized memory against frozen longitudinal investor trajectories. Its generation pipeline combines deterministic, theory-informed impact rules, controlled LLM narration, and automated quality screening; a Post-Shock checkpoint isolates whether an agent has integrated a material event into its persistent user model. On 2,994 questions from 276 personas, seven frontier LLMs and up to seven memory configurations remain far from saturated: no full-context configuration exceeds approximately 0.47 overall accuracy or approximately 39% on multiple-choice questions. Attribution analysis shows that summary-based memory often preserves factual details while losing the preference signals needed for personalization; simple retrieval can therefore outperform purpose-built memory systems, with the gap widening after shocks.

</details>


### [117] [BrainBench: Benchmarking Large Language Models for Comprehensive EEG Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04156)
*Yangxuan Zhou,Sha Zhao,Yuning Chen,Chen Wu,Jiquan Wang,Shijian Li,Gang Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个统一的基准测试集（benchmark），用于全面评估大语言模型在"指令条件化的脑电图理解"任务上的能力。该基准包含四个子集（基础分析、睡眠评估、神经认知评估、生理整合），覆盖17个数据集、数十个任务和大量真实数据实例，通过多种验证方式（数值、分类、集合、序列、语义、工件验证）评估模型输出的科学报告和工件质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有脑电图（EEG）分析评估主要针对孤立的解码任务或单一系统演示，未能充分量化大语言模型（LLM）在从自然语言指令到信号处理、定量证据和科学解释的完整工作流中的综合能力。作者将此能力定义为"全面EEG理解"，并指出现有评估缺乏统一的基准来系统衡量该能力。

Method: 提出了一个统一的基准测试集，包含四个子集：基础分析、睡眠评估、神经认知评估和生理整合。覆盖17个数据集、多个任务和大量真实数据实例。系统需根据指令对EEG记录（可含可选的生理信号）执行分析并生成科学报告及所需工件。输出通过六种验证方式评估：数值、分类、集合、序列、语义和工件验证。作者在两种执行范式下评估多个代表性LLM：(1) 自主代码执行的CodeAct；(2) 结构化智能体分析的BrainAgent，总共超过10万次执行。

Result: 不同模型、子集、难度级别和执行范式之间的评估结果差异显著。结果表明EEG能力取决于模型本身及其操作化方式（即如何将LLM用于执行分析任务），这证实了统一的基准测试对于全面评估LLM驱动的EEG理解能力是必要的。

Conclusion: 该基准为推进基于LLM的EEG理解提供了一个可复现的测试平台。代码和基准将很快发布，评估结果将持续更新。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) analysis extends beyond assigning predefined labels to recordings; it requires workflows connecting natural-language instructions, signal processing, quantitative evidence, and scientific interpretation. We term this capability \emph{comprehensive EEG understanding}. Existing evaluations, however, primarily target isolated decoding tasks or system-specific demonstrations, leaving the competence of large language models (LLMs) insufficiently quantified. We introduce \benchmarkname{}, a unified benchmark for comprehensive, instruction-conditioned EEG understanding. It comprises four subsets---Foundational Analysis, Sleep Assessment, Neurocognitive Assessment, and Physiological Integration---covering 17 datasets, \numcases{} tasks, and over \numinstances{} real-data instances. Given an instruction and EEG recordings with optional physiological signals, a system must perform the analysis and produce a scientifically grounded report and, when required, artifacts. Outputs are assessed through numerical, categorical, set, sequence, semantic, and artifact validation. We evaluate \nummodels{} representative LLMs across more than 100K executions under two paradigms: autonomous code execution with CodeAct and structured agentic analysis with BrainAgent. Results vary substantially across models, subsets, difficulty levels, and execution paradigms, showing that EEG competence depends on the model and its operationalization. \benchmarkname{} provides a reproducible testbed for advancing LLM-based EEG understanding. The code and benchmark will be released soon, with evaluation results continuously updated.

</details>


### [118] [Adversarially Robust Abductive Fusion of Pre-trained Transformer-based Perception Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04190)
*Mario Leiva,Yue Ma,Qinru Qiu,Gerardo Simari,Paulo Shakarian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需领域知识的元认知层（基于标签向量池LVP的几何规则），用于检测和纠正多个预训练感知模型在分布偏移下的错误。该方法在不需要任何手工领域知识的情况下，与依赖领域知识的规则性能相当（F1差距在0.002以内），并且在对抗性标签翻转攻击下显著优于多数投票基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练感知模型在分布偏移下准确率下降，单纯集成多个模型无法恢复性能（多数投票牺牲召回换取精度，且对协调性故障脆弱）。已有的元认知方法依赖手工编写的领域知识线索（如物体尺寸先验、分割掩码），但这些知识无法迁移到真正新颖的场景。因此需要一种不依赖领域知识、可迁移到任意新颖环境的错误检测层。

Method: 核心思想是利用向量空间几何：为每个模型构建Label Vector Pools (LVP)，即从各模型自身训练嵌入中获得的标签向量池。通过检测结果相对于训练确定的原型的几何关系，推导出错误检测规则。该方法保持神经符号特性，几何规则与领域知识规则共享统一的逻辑框架，可在有领域知识时互补增强。在测试时，将多个不完美ViT检测器的融合建模为基于一致性的溯因问题，通过精确整数规划(IP)和多项式时间启发式求解。

Result: 在包含15个天气偏移测试集和六个ViT检测器的航空影像基准上：无领域知识层在干净数据上与最强多数投票变体性能相当（F1差距在0.005以内）；在90%标签翻转攻击下平均F1为0.42，优于MV-Plurality的0.35（相对提升22%）；当翻转率超过0.4时，在所有测试集上均取得最高F1。

Conclusion: 实验表明，元认知错误检测层可以在完全不需要领域知识的前提下，仅通过模型自身训练嵌入的几何结构学习到有效的错误检测规则，在分布偏移和对抗性攻击下表现出鲁棒性，同时保持神经符号框架的灵活性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Deploying pre-trained perception models in novel environments degrades their accuracy under distributional shift, and assembling them alone does not recover it: combiners such as majority voting trade recall for precision and are brittle to coordinated failures. Prior metacognitive methods learn logical rules that flag a model's errors, but rely on hand-authored domain-knowledge cues (object-size priors, segmentation masks) that do not transfer to genuinely novel scenes. We show that this metacognitive layer can be learned without any domain knowledge by exploiting vector-space geometry: per-model Label Vector Pools (LVP), built from each model's own training embeddings, yield error-detection rules from the geometry of detections relative to training-determined prototypes, reaching parity with domain-knowledge rules to within $0.002$ every F1 on test set. Because the approach remains neurosymbolic, these geometric rules share a single logical framework and can still be complemented by domain knowledge when available. We frame the fusion of multiple imperfect ViT-based detectors as a consistency-based abduction problem solved at test time by an exact Integer Program (IP) and a polynomial-time heuristic. On an aerial-imagery benchmark of 15 weather-shifted test sets and six ViT detectors, our domain-knowledge-free layer matches the strongest majority-vote variant on clean data (within $0.005$ F1) and, unlike every majority-vote baseline, retains its performance under a coordinated label-flipping attack: at a $90\%$ flip rate it averages $0.42$ F1 versus $0.35$ for MV-Plurality (a $22\%$ relative gain) and attains the highest F1 on \emph{every} test set once the flip rate exceeds $0.4$

</details>


### [119] [MatrAIx: Simulating the World with 8.3 Billion Persona Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04205)
*Xiaomin Li,Yuexing Hao,Jianheng Hou,Jintao Huang,Qianfeng Wen,Shirley Huang,Yifan Liu,Xiaoyi Liu,Yilan Fan,Yijun Wang,Koutian Wu,Ruoqi Gao,Muhammad Ahmed Mohsin,Jing Tang,Brihi Joshi,Heming Liu,Zheyuan Deng,Zonglin Di,Sankalp Jajee,Jiuyao Lu,Zhiwei Zhang,Saksham Kapoor,Ishan Gupta,Yunhan Zhao,Chanwoo Park,Yucheng Lu,Bing Hu,Weihang Xiao,Aravind Mohan,Hanwen Xing,Runyu Zhang,Mihir Kulshreshtha,Yuanda Xu,Qianyu Zhu,Dianzhuo Wang,Yuxin Xiao,Bowen Jiang,Yongye Su,Wenhao Chai,Zuxin Liu,Lawrence Yunliang Chen,Xuandong Zhao,Ethan Ye,Shivam Patel,Jason Xie,Alex Martin Richmond,Weixiang Ding,Emre Okcular,Diya Mathew,Ziheng Wang,Rana M. Shahroz Khan,Zhejian Peng,Fang Wu,Fan Nie,Xinyang Han,Yubin Kim,Jiawei Zhang,Zhenting Qi,Huangyuan Su,Xu Pan,Abinitha Gourabathina,Hyewon Jeong,Hemanth Neelgund Ramesh,Kumail Alhamoud,Kimia Hamidieh,Zidi Xiong,Samuel Schmidgall,Pengrui Han,Yepeng Huang,Yongheng Wang,Bowen Yang,Alex Gu,Yuchu Wang,Akshay Paruchuri,Brenna Li,Hejie Cui,Jiayuan Ding,Chaosheng Dong,Jiahao Wang,Yixuan He,Chi Wang,Pamela Bhattacharya,Tianyi Peng,Paul Pu Liang,Mitchell Gordon,Yilun Du,Marinka Zitnik,James Zou,Prasanna Tambe,Philip Torr,Emily Fox,Asu Ozdaglar,Dawn Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了MatrAIx，一个大规模模拟用户评估基础设施，包含83亿条用户档案（Persona 8B）、四个交互测试环境（问卷、AI聊天机器人、网页、应用）、1010个跨25个领域的应用任务，以及18000多次评估试验。该系统通过异构LLM驱动的角色代理来评估AI系统和数字产品，在400次受控试验中实现了91.5%的角色遵循率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类评估AI系统和数字产品成本高昂、速度慢且难以规模化，而离线评估虽然可扩展但往往忽略了人类多样性和交互行为。因此需要一个既能规模化又能保留用户异构性和交互性的评估基础设施。

Method: MatrAIx包含三个核心组件：(1) Persona 8B包含83亿条角色记录，由1290个分类维度表示，通过依赖图采样或人工撰写获取，并发布约百万条质量过滤的核心数据集；(2) MatrAIx Playground提供Survey、AI Chatbot、Web和App四种评估环境；(3) 提供1010个跨25个以上领域的应用任务。评估使用三种LLM（Claude Opus 4.8、GPT 5.5、Claude Haiku 4.5）驱动角色代理，并进行了两项主要验证研究。

Result: 共进行了18189次评估试验，覆盖八个代表性任务。反馈显示决策和偏好随角色背景而变化，包括提价后的犹豫、AI助手失败后的继续意愿以及延迟容忍度。400次受控试验中91.5%（366/400）正确表达或抑制了声明行为。人和LLM法官对人资角色提取质量进行了评估。

Conclusion: MatrAIx提供了一个端到端的评估基础设施，能够以多样化的模拟人类用户对AI系统和数字产品进行大规模评估，在可扩展性和用户异构性之间取得了平衡。

Abstract: Human evaluation of AI systems and digital products is costly, slow, and difficult to scale. Offline evaluations are more scalable but often abstract away human diversity and interactive behavior. We therefore introduce MatrAIx, a population-scale simulated-user evaluation infrastructure for testing AI systems and digital products with heterogeneous users. MatrAIx has three core components: First, Persona 8B contains 8.3 billion persona records represented by 1,290 categorical dimensions. Records are either sampled from a dependency graph that preserves correlated attributes or derived from human-authored profiles. We release a quality-filtered coreset of approximately 1 million personas, comprising 599,847 human-grounded and 400,000 synthetic records. Second, the MatrAIx Playground provides four environments in which diverse users evaluate and interact with digital products: Survey, AI Chatbot, Web, and App. Third, MatrAIx provides 1,010 application tasks spanning more than 25 domains, including Commerce, Software, Finance, and Healthcare. We conducted 18,189 evaluation trials across eight representative tasks. Persona agents were powered by three LLMs: Claude Opus 4.8, GPT 5.5, and Claude Haiku 4.5. The resulting feedback captures how decisions and preferences vary across persona backgrounds, including hesitation after a price increase, willingness to continue after an AI assistant fails, and latency tolerance. We conducted two main validation studies: First, a 400-trial controlled study evaluated persona adherence across ten behavioral attributes and all four environments. The declared behavior was expressed or correctly suppressed in 366 trials (91.5%). Second, human and LLM judges evaluated the extraction quality of human-grounded personas. Overall, MatrAIx provides an end-to-end infrastructure for evaluating AI systems and digital products with diverse simulated human users.

</details>


### [120] [Interoceptive Attention as Dynamic Homeostatic Prioritization in a Foraging Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04232)
*St John Grimbly,Nicolas Kuske,Evert A. Boonstra,Bruce A. Bassett,Charel van Hoof,Rowan Hodson,Benjamin Rosman,Ryan Smith,Mark Solms,Jonathan P. Shock*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究在有限感知带宽下，主动推理代理如何将内感受精度预算动态分配到最需要的通道。在AffectWorld四通道觅食网格世界中，选择性分配相比均匀精度分配使学习期存活率提升一倍以上（0.414 vs 0.199），且该收益同时作用于感知和规划过程，并具有需求对齐特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物系统必须在有限感知带宽下调节互相竞争的需求，提高某一估计的精度会牺牲其他估计的精度。因此任何固定预算系统都需要决定如何分配感知精度资源，这引出了精度分配策略的机制性问题。

Method: 在主动推理框架下构建一个需维持多种身体需求的觅食代理。代理每步读取自身身体状态信念，识别最需要的通道，并将固定的内感受精度预算重新分配至该通道，使同一精度塑形的似然同时用于信念更新和规划。实验环境为四通道觅食网格世界AffectWorld，对照条件包括均匀精度分配代理和反向（最少需求通道）分配代理。

Result: 选择性精度分配在11种布局、32随机种子下，学习期存活率是对均匀精度代理的两倍以上（0.414 vs 0.199，配对聚类bootstrap p≤1e-4）。若仅从规划器中去除精度塑形似然，收益约损失一半，说明收益同时作用于感知与规划。将精度指向最少需求通道的效果差于均匀分配，证明精度分配与需求对齐。被注意通道的动力学学习速度约为均匀条件的两倍，且在匹配观测次数后仍保持领先，该行为痕迹体现在学习速度而非存活率中。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在主动推理代理中，按需动态分配感知精度能显著提升生存表现，其收益机制经由感知和规划双路径传递，且与需求对齐性紧密相关。精度路由不仅带来存活率提升，还在被注意通道上留下更快的动力学学习痕迹。

Abstract: Biological systems must regulate competing needs under limited perceptual bandwidth, where sharpening one estimate costs the capacity to sharpen the others. Any fixed-budget system therefore has to decide where to allocate its perceptual precision. We study this in a foraging agent that must keep several bodily needs satisfied to survive, modelled with active inference. At each step it reads its own body-state beliefs, identifies the most-needed channel, and reallocates a fixed budget of interoceptive precision toward it, so that the same precision-shaped likelihood feeds both belief update and planning. In AffectWorld, a four-channel foraging gridworld, this selective allocation more than doubles learning-phase survival at matched budget against a uniform-precision agent ($0.414$ vs $0.199$ across 11 layouts, $n{=}32$ seeds each, paired cluster-bootstrap $p \leq 10^{-4}$). Two further results sharpen the mechanism. The benefit runs through planning as well as perception, since denying the shaped likelihood to the planner alone removes about half of it. It is also need-aligned, since aiming precision at the least-needed channel does worse than spreading it evenly. The attended channel additionally learns its own dynamics about twice as fast, and stays ahead even at matched observation count, a behavioural trace of the same precision routing, visible in learning speed, not survival.

</details>


### [121] [The RAIL Principles for Neurosymbolic AI: Reasoning, Assurances, Interfacing and Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04285)
*Agnese Chiatti,Michael Cochez,Cristina Cornelio,Sebastijan Dumancic,Artur d'Avila Garcez,Luis C. Lamb,Lia Morra,Mathias Niepert,Robert Peharz,Alberto Speranzon,Maarten Stol,Annette Ten Teije,Thiviyan Thanapalasingam,Frank Van Harmelen,Emile Van Krieken,Antonio Vergari,Benjie Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出神经符号AI（Neurosymbolic AI）的四个设计原则——RAIL（Reasoning, Assurances, Interfacing, Learning），认为机器学习和形式推理的结合并非小众方法，而是构建可靠、高效、可信AI系统的关键路径。RAIL框架可统一分析多种看似不同的AI系统，从物理感知学习到Alpha-*系列、因果学习和工具增强的大语言模型，为工程师提供更理性的系统设计决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI系统设计缺乏统一的理论视角来审视机器学习和符号推理的结合方式。作者认为神经符号AI不是边缘方法，而是构建高可靠、高可信AI系统的核心手段，需要一套统一的原则来指导设计、分析和部署。

Method: 提出RAIL四原则框架（Reasoning推理、Assurances保障、Interfacing接口、Learning学习），并用该框架对多种主流AI系统进行统一分析，包括物理感知机器学习、神经引导搜索（如AlphaGo/AlphaZero等）、因果学习及工具增强的大语言模型，验证框架的解释力和指导价值。

Result: RAIL框架能够统一刻画看似互不相关的AI系统架构，揭示它们在神经符号设计上的共性。该框架可帮助工程师在设计生产级AI系统时做出更有依据、更原则化的决策，并为新一代AI技术整合神经符号方法提供实践指导。

Conclusion: 神经符号AI是发展可靠、高效、可信AI系统的基础性路径。RAIL框架为理解和设计AI系统提供了统一视角，应成为AI工程师的重要设计工具，推动下一代AI技术的落地应用。

Abstract: Neurosymbolic AI systems that integrate machine learning and symbolic reasoning are rapidly gaining attention. They complement the data-intensive statistical approaches of neural networks and language models with symbolic reasoning algorithms to function in high-stakes domains or in low-data regimes that characterize many real-world applications. We argue that the neurosymbolic combination of machine learning and formal reasoning is not a niche approach within AI, but rather includes many already successful techniques that are of crucial importance to the development of reliable, efficient and, ultimately, trustworthy systems. This perspective prompts a re-examination of the design of current AI systems. We show that many leading AI systems, including some that are not traditionally considered as neurosymbolic, can be analysed from the perspective of four principles of neurosymbolic AI design: Reasoning, Assurances, Interfacing and Learning (RAIL). Applying the RAIL framework offers a unified view of seemingly disparate AI systems, ranging from physics-aware machine learning to neuro-guided search (such as Google DeepMind's Alpha-* suite), causal learning and tool-augmented Large Language Models. Importantly, the RAIL principles will enable engineers to make better-informed and more principled decisions about the design and deployment of production-level AI systems. In this article, we introduce the RAIL principles, examine how they can be applied across major areas of AI, and illustrate how they may guide practitioners to integrate neurosymbolic methods into next-generation AI technologies.

</details>


### [122] [SafeCommit: Certifying When Memory-Grounded Agents May Safely Act](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04289)
*Mayur Akewar,Ravi Ranjan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SafeCommit，一个位于智能体推理与外部执行之间的风险控制层，通过构建校准的潜在世界集合并生成合形动作证书，确保长程智能体在记忆不确定性下不会过早承诺执行具有外部副作用的动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程智能体依赖持久记忆和工具执行具有外部副作用的动作，但其记忆基础可能过时、冲突、不完整或被破坏。过早承诺（premature commitment）是核心失败模式，即智能体在未确认记忆可靠性之前就采取行动。

Method: SafeCommit构建一个校准的潜在世界集合，融合记忆、观测、工具输出、溯源和策略约束。仅当合形动作证书（conformal action certificate）证明动作在所有保留世界中都是安全的时才允许执行；否则选择低副作用探测动作或保守回退。在校准的世界覆盖下，不安全认证提交概率受限于目标水平α，并可将校准误差与表征误差分离。

Result: 提供了一个无依赖的受控模拟器，展示了安全性与效用之间的权衡，并可用一条命令复现所有结果。

Conclusion: 本文的核心贡献是为智能体提供一种具体方法，不仅决定应该做什么，还决定何时现有证据足以安全地执行。

Abstract: Long-horizon agents increasingly use persistent memory and tools to take actions with external side effects. A central failure mode is premature commitment: an agent acts before resolving whether its memory grounding is stale, conflicting, incomplete, or corrupted. We formalize this problem as safe commitment under memory uncertainty and introduce SafeCommit, a risk controlled layer between agent reasoning and external execution. The layer constructs a calibrated set of plausible latent worlds from memory, observations, tool outputs, provenance, and policy constraints. It permits a side effectful action only when a conformal action certificate shows that the action is safe in every retained world. Otherwise, it selects a low-side-effect probe that targets the worlds blocking certification, or returns a conservative fallback. Under calibrated world coverage, the probability of an unsafe certified commit is at most the target level α; with imperfect world proposal, the bound separates calibration and representation error. A dependency-free controlled simulator illustrates the safety-utility tradeoff and reproduces all reported results with one command. The goal is to offer a concrete approach for deciding not only what an agent should do, but when the available evidence is sufficient to safely do it.

</details>


### [123] [NeuMoSync: End-to-End Neuromodulatory Control for Plasticity and Adaptability in Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04358)
*Seyed Roozbeh Razavi Rohani,Khashayar Khajavi,Wesley Chung,Mandana Samiei,Mo Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出NeuMoSync，一种受大脑全局神经调节机制启发的架构，通过为每个神经元引入可学习的特征向量和更高抽象层次的调节模块，动态调控神经元的激活和突触可塑性，以解决持续学习中的可塑性损失和知识迁移不足问题。在多种持续学习基准上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习要求模型顺序学习多个任务，但深度神经网络常面临可塑性损失（plasticity loss）和知识迁移能力差的问题，这会严重阻碍模型的长期适应性。现有方法未能充分解决这些根本性挑战。

Method: NeuMoSync架构包含两个核心组件：（1）每个神经元配备可学习的特征向量，用于追踪网络范围的历史上下文；（2）一个运行在更高抽象层次的模块，基于当前输入和网络演化状态合成神经元特异的调节信号，动态调控激活动力学和突触可塑性。

Result: 在多种持续学习基准上评估，包括记忆（Random Label CIFAR-10/MNIST）、概念漂移（Shuffle CIFAR-10/Mini-ImageNet）、类增量学习（Class Split ImageNet/CIFAR-100）和域增量学习（Permuted MNIST）。NeuMoSync在保持可塑性方面表现出色，在前向和后向适应性上均优于现有方法。消融实验验证了各组件的必要性，可学习的调节信号分析揭示了跨任务的可解释协调模式。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了将全局协调机制集成到深度学习系统中的潜力，有助于推进稳健、自适应的持续学习。代码已公开。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) requires models to learn tasks sequentially, yet deep neural networks often suffer from plasticity loss and poor knowledge transfer, which can impede their long-term adaptability. Drawing high-level inspiration from global neuromodulatory mechanisms in the brain, we introduce Neuromodulation and Synchronization (NeuMoSync), a novel architecture that integrates dynamic, neuron-specific modulation into deep neural networks to enhance their adaptability and plasticity. NeuMoSync extends standard neural network architectures with learnable feature vectors for each neuron that track network-wide historical context and with a module operating at a higher level of abstraction. This module synthesizes neuron-specific signals, conditioned on both current inputs and the network's evolving state, to adaptively regulate activation dynamics and synaptic plasticity. Evaluated on diverse CL benchmarks, including memorization (Random Label CIFAR-10 and Random Label MNIST), concept drift (Shuffle CIFAR-10 and Shuffle Mini-ImageNet), class-incremental learning (Class Split ImageNet and Class Split CIFAR-100), and domain-incremental learning (Permuted MNIST), NeuMoSync demonstrates strong performance in retaining plasticity and achieves improvements in both forward and backward adaptation compared with existing methods. Ablation studies validate the necessity of each component, while analysis of the learned modulatory signals reveals interpretable coordination patterns across tasks. Our work underscores the potential of integrating global coordination mechanisms into deep learning systems to advance robust, adaptive continual learning. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/RoozbehRazavi/NeuMoSync.

</details>


### [124] [Improving Auto-Design of Neural PDE Solvers with a Domain-Specific Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04384)
*Shengxin Kong,Liwen Xu,Jingwen Fu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ADSL-PDE通过引入结构化搜索状态表示，将神经PDE求解器的自动设计从代码级搜索提升到设计决策级搜索，由确定性编译器将有效状态映射为可执行求解器，使进化智能体在10次迭代内实现超过52%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经PDE求解器自动设计的根本问题是搜索空间表示问题。在无限制的Python程序空间中，有效求解器的密度极低——大多数候选程序存在语法错误、语义不兼容或数值不稳定问题。直接代码生成迫使LLM将大部分搜索能力浪费在处理实现失败上，而非推理求解器质量本身。因此需要一个能抽象掉底层实现细节、聚焦于功能性设计决策的搜索空间。

Method: ADSL-PDE引入概念层与可执行代码之间的结构化搜索状态，该状态表示决定神经PDE求解器功能的关键维度（架构、物理约束、目标函数、采样策略和优化方法），同时抽象掉底层实现细节。一个确定性编译器将每个有效的搜索状态映射为可执行的求解器，从而重塑搜索空间：移除大量无效程序区域、提高有意义的候选密度，并保留组合自由以发现未见过的设计。基于此表示，进化智能体通过经验反馈迭代地提出、评估和精炼求解器搜索状态。

Result: 在多个PDE基准测试中，ADSL-PDE同时提升了搜索效率和优化稳定性，在前十次进化迭代内实现了超过52%的性能改进。

Conclusion: 结果表明，LLM驱动的自动设计环境中，有效的智能体不仅需要更强的推理能力，更需要一种能将探索集中于有效且关键决策上的搜索表示。这一更广泛的原则对LLM驱动的自动设计具有通用意义。

Abstract: Neural PDE solver auto-design is fundamentally a search-space representation problem. In the space of unrestricted Python programs, valid solvers form an extremely sparse subset: most candidate programs are syntactically incorrect, semantically incompatible, or numerically unstable. Direct code generation therefore forces an LLM to spend most of its search capacity navigating implementation failures rather than reasoning about solver quality. ADSL-PDE addresses this challenge by introducing a structured search state between solver concepts and executable code. It represents the functional decisions that determine a neural PDE solver (architecture, physical constraints, objectives, sampling, and optimization) while abstracting away low-level implementation details. A deterministic compiler maps each valid search state to an executable solver. In effect, ADSL-PDE reshapes the search space: it removes large regions of invalid programs, increases the density of meaningful candidates, and preserves the compositional freedom needed to discover previously unseen designs. Solver evolution can thus operate over design decisions rather than code artifacts. Built on this representation, our evolutionary agent iteratively proposes, evaluates, and refines solver search states using empirical feedback. Across multiple PDE benchmarks, ADSL-PDE improves both search efficiency and optimization stability, achieving an improvement of more than 52% within the first ten evolution iterations. These results suggest a broader principle for LLM-driven auto-design: effective agents do not merely require stronger reasoning, but rather a search representation that concentrates exploration on valid and consequential decisions.

</details>


### [125] [Architectural Implications of Agentic AI Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04458)
*Jirong Yang,Peizhe Liu,Chaojie Zhang,Jovan Stojkovic*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次对数据中心中的智能体AI（Agentic AI）工作负载进行架构级刻画。通过在微软Azure的生产研究和开源框架的受控研究，揭示了智能体执行具有碎片化和异构性的特征：请求扩展为跨CPU-GPU边界的LLM推理、工具调用和编排决策的工作流。针对传统统一服务器的架构不匹配问题，作者提出了Agora原型系统，通过动态回收空闲CPU核、GPU内存超订与状态预取、按角色划分核池和亲和性感知调度等机制，在保护智能体尾部延迟的同时提升利用率和吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体AI正快速进入数据中心，但其对服务器架构的影响尚未被探索。传统服务器为统一、均匀的负载设计，而智能体工作负载呈现出碎片化和异构性，导致架构不匹配和资源浪费，亟需理解其特性并设计适配的服务器架构。

Method: 方法包括两部分：一是对智能体工作负载进行架构表征，提出智能体工作流分类法（taxonomy），结合微软Azure的生产研究和开源框架的受控研究来分析执行结构、模型组合、任务多样性对资源需求的影响；二是基于这些发现设计Agora原型系统，包含四个关键机制：动态回收空闲CPU核用于共置的吞吐量工作、GPU内存超订（每GPU放置更多智能体并预取下一位智能体状态以隐藏交换延迟）、按角色划分核池并采用亲和性感知调度恢复局部性、以及根据工作负载自动调优机制参数。

Result: 研究发现智能体执行碎片化且异构：请求扩展为LLM推理、工具调用和编排决策的工作流，反复跨越CPU-GPU边界；CPU位于关键路径上；负载随时间保持低位但出现突发尖峰；模型组合决定GPU使用均匀性；任务和工具的多样性进一步扩大资源需求范围。Agora在这些发现指导下，显著提高利用率和服务器吞吐量，同时保持智能体尾部延迟。

Conclusion: 智能体AI的工作负载特征与传统统一服务器架构存在根本性不匹配，需要针对智能体工作负载重新设计服务器架构。Agora验证了通过动态资源管理（CPU回收、GPU内存超订、按角色分区调度）可以有效提升能体服务器的效率和性能，同时为未来智能体AI服务器架构指明了关键方向。

Abstract: Agentic AI is emerging in datacenters, but its architectural implications remain unexplored. We organize agentic workflows in a taxonomy and present its first architectural characterization with a production study at Microsoft Azure and a controlled study of open-source frameworks. We show that agentic execution is fragmented and heterogeneous. Requests expand into a workflow of LLM inferences, tool invocations, and orchestration decisions that repeatedly cross the CPU-GPU boundary. Our taxonomy explains how this fragmentation turns into resource demand. As orchestration and tools run on the host, the CPU sits on the critical path. Execution structure sets the load over time, which stays low with sudden spikes. Model composition sets how evenly the workflow uses the GPUs. Diversity in tasks and tools widens this range even further. These characteristics expose architectural mismatches of conventional uniform servers. Fragmented execution strands CPU and GPU capacity despite bursty demand. Different software roles make homogeneous CPU provisioning inefficient. Finally, multiplexing many agents onto shared cores degrades microarchitectural locality. Guided by our findings, we derive implications for agentic servers and examine them through Agora, our prototype for commodity servers. Agora dynamically harvests idle CPU cores for co-located throughput work, while protecting agentic tail latency against tool spikes. It oversubscribes GPU memory by placing more agents on each GPU, prefetching the next agent's state to hide swap latency. To match the machine to the heterogeneous roles, Agora pools cores by role and applies affinity-aware scheduling to restore locality. It automatically tunes mechanisms to the workload. Agora improves utilization and server throughput while preserving agent tail latency. Our insights also identify key directions for future server architectures for agentic AI.

</details>


### [126] [CARGO-VL: Counterfactual Arbitration with Risk-Constrained Group Optimization for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04509)
*De Jiang,Zhengyang Zhang,Kehong Yuan,Shaohua Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CARGO-VL，一种基于组相对优化的框架，用于多模态视觉-语言系统中可信证据仲裁。它将匹配变体（对齐、仅图像正确、仅文本正确、两者错误的A/V/T/N证据状态）作为一个捆绑进行联合优化，结合条件正确性、转换奖励和原始-对偶控制器来平衡不安全答案与过度放弃。该框架改善了冲突处理、不支持答案的避免和模态平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言系统将图像与检索文本结合，但这些来源可能相互矛盾或共同无法支持答案。可靠模型需要识别可信来源并在两者都不充分时放弃回答。现有的后训练目标独立评分样本，没有在反事实证据变化下强制执行一致行为，因此缺乏对证据冲突场景的显式建模。

Method: CARGO-VL采用组相对优化框架：1) 将四种匹配变体（对齐A、仅图像正确V、仅文本正确T、两者错误N）作为一组联合优化；2) 目标函数耦合条件正确性与三种转换奖励——答案不变性、来源等变性、答案到放弃的切换；3) 使用原始-对偶控制器在"不安全答案"与"过度放弃"之间进行自适应平衡。同时贡献XMC（扩展模态冲突）四条件冲突训练数据集，并在CMC-Bench和Modality-Bias上评估迁移。

Result: 在多种随机种子下，CARGO-VL在冲突处理、不支持答案规避和模态平衡方面均优于逐点基线。消融实验表明，关系转换信号和自适应风险控制提供互补收益。

Conclusion: 反事实一致性可作为多模态可靠证据仲裁的实际优化目标。组相对的关系信号与自适应风险控制相结合，能够有效提升系统在冲突和证据不足场景下的可靠性行为。

Abstract: Vision-language systems combine images with retrieved text, but these sources can disagree or jointly fail to support an answer. Reliable models must identify the trustworthy source and abstain when neither is adequate. Existing post-training objectives score instances independently and therefore do not enforce coherent behavior under counterfactual evidence changes. We introduce CARGO-VL, a group-relative framework that optimizes matched variants covering aligned, image-correct, text-correct, and both-wrong (A/V/T/N) evidence states as one bundle. Its objective couples condition-wise correctness with transition rewards for answer invariance, source equivariance, and answer-to-abstention switching, while a primal-dual controller balances unsafe answers against excessive deferral. We also contribute XMC (eXtended Modal Conflict), a four-condition conflict training resource, and evaluate transfer on CMC-Bench and Modality-Bias. Across multiple seeds, CARGO-VL improves conflict handling, unsupported-answer avoidance, and modality balance over pointwise baselines. Ablations identify complementary benefits from relational transition signals and adaptive risk control, supporting counterfactual consistency as a practical objective for reliable multimodal evidence arbitration.

</details>


### [127] [Leak-Resistant Unlearning: A New Benchmark for Evaluating Multi-Hop Reasoning Consistency and Recovery Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04519)
*Haoting Qian,Qingjie Zhang,Zhicong Huang,Cheng Hong,Han Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出UNLEARNING基准，用于系统评估LLM机器遗忘的鲁棒性，涵盖多样化的多跳推理路径和恢复攻击场景。实验表明现有遗忘方法在这些场景下均存在脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遗忘基准主要包含单跳问题和有限的多跳问题，存在两个关键挑战：(1)知识并非孤立存在，多样的多跳推理路径可能比普通查询更容易导致知识泄露；(2)遗忘可能具有脆弱性——被遗忘的知识可以通过轻量级后遗忘适应等恢复攻击部分恢复，导致静态评估不够充分。

Method: 论文提出了UNLEARNING基准框架，涵盖多样的多跳推理路径和恢复攻击场景。在3个模型、6种遗忘方法和2个精心整理的数据集上进行系统实验评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，现有遗忘方法对多跳推理路径和恢复攻击均表现出脆弱性，即被遗忘的知识可以通过多样化推理路径或攻击手段被重新获取。

Conclusion: 论文进一步探索了LLM遗忘中遗忘质量、鲁棒性和模型效用三者之间的权衡关系，为设计更健壮的机器遗忘方法提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Benchmarking machine unlearning methods is critical to understand whether sensitive knowledge is removed from large language models (LLMs) or not. Current unlearning benchmarks include mainly single-hop questions and a narrow set of multi-hop questions. Although effective, they still face two challenges. (1) Knowledge is not isolated, whereby diverse multi-hop reasoning paths can potentially induce knowledge leakage than normal queries. (2) Unlearning may be fragile: unlearned knowledge can be partially recovered through recovery attacks such as lightweight post-unlearning adaptation, making static evaluation insufficient. Therefore, in this paper, we introduce \unlearning as a novel benchmark to understand robust LLM knowledge removal across diverse reasoning paths and recovery attacks. We experiment with this benchmark on 3 models, 6 unlearning methods, and 2 carefully curated datasets. Results show that existing methods are vulnerable to multi-hop reasoning paths and recovery attacks. We further explore the trade-off among forget quality, robustness, and model utility for LLM unlearning.

</details>


### [128] [What Is a Skill Worth? Structure-Aware Shapley Valuation of Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04562)
*Tao Li,Junfeng Liu,Qinghua Zhao,Yifan Li,Lei Wang,Bo Shao,Xuejun Liu,Linjun Shou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillSV，一种结构感知的Shapley式技能估值框架，用于在固定智能体和既有任务分布下，为技能的内部单元（规则、示例、脚本、启发式等）分配信用。SkillSV将技能编译为单元、依赖和层级结构，仅评估有效的反事实技能，使用配对删除和长度中立填充分离内容价值与上下文成本，并通过带预算的回滚估计器处理噪声智能体评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体技能越来越通过自动化反馈循环进行优化，产生冗长的结构化产物，但其内部单元的价值仍然不明确。现有数据或提示跨度估值方法不适用于技能估值，因为技能单元具有结构化特征：单元间存在依赖关系、属于文档层级、可触发智能体行为、且消耗有限的提示上下文。

Method: SkillSV首先将技能编译为单元、依赖关系和层级结构，确保只评估有效的反事实技能（避免破坏结构约束的无效删除）；采用配对删除与长度中立填充技术，将内容价值与上下文成本解耦；使用带回滚预算的估计器来应对智能体评估中的噪声。

Result: 在四个智能体基准上评估了SkillSV的忠实性、可操作性和可解释性：能够恢复单元交互、保持聚合技能提升、并指导安全的剪枝与压缩。

Conclusion: SkillSV是一种有效的结构感知技能估值方法，能够为智能体技能的内部单元提供可靠的信用分配，支持技能的安全剪枝和压缩，提升技能优化的可解释性和可操作性。

Abstract: Agent skills are increasingly optimized by automated feedback loops, producing long structured artifacts whose internal value remains unclear. We study skill valuation: assigning credit to the internal units of a fixed skill, such as rules, examples, scripts, and heuristics, under a fixed agent and held-out task distribution. Skill valuation differs from data or prompt-span valuation because skill units are structured: they may depend on other units, belong to a document hierarchy, trigger agent behavior, and consume limited prompt context. We introduce SkillSV, a structure-aware Shapley-style framework for skill valuation. SkillSV compiles a skill into units, dependencies, and hierarchy, so that only valid counterfactual skills are evaluated. It uses paired deletion and length-neutral padding to separate content value from context cost, and estimates the resulting values with a rollout-budgeted estimator for noisy agent evaluations. On four agentic benchmarks, we assess the faithfulness, actionability, and explanation of SkillSV: it recovers unit interactions, preserves aggregate skill lift, and guides safe pruning and compression.

</details>


### [129] [Joint UAV Flight and Opportunistic Routing under Reinforcement Learning for Delay-Tolerant Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04590)
*Xiao Wang,Shun-Ren Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出JUROR框架，基于PPO联合优化UAV飞行航向与机会路由决策，采用集中式训练、分散式执行（CTDE）范式，在间歇连通下提升DTN端到端传输性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 延迟容忍网络（DTN）在稀疏连通场景下，间歇接触、有限缓冲和消息TTL限制导致投递稀疏与拥塞，使端到端性能严重下降。现有方案未能联合优化路由与可控的UAV航向以主动创造未来接触机会。

Method: 将问题建模为因子化POMDP，包含序贯运动-路由耦合与逐步团队奖励；基于CTDE架构，分散式actor依据局部观测决策，训练期critic使用全局统计量；采用PPO框架，并可选多时间跨度热点预测器提供辅助监督，实现UAV离散航向与每节点复制策略的联合优化。

Result: 在四种流量模式下的仿真结果表明，JUROR在保持接触受限的分散式执行能力的同时，相较于PRoPHET和MaxProp基准协议取得了有效的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过集中式训练与分散式执行的协同因子化路由-UAV控制架构，可在接触受限条件下同时提升投递率、降低延迟并缓解拥塞，验证了PPO框架在DTN联合优化中的有效性。

Abstract: The growing deployment of delay-tolerant networks (DTNs) has made store-carry-forward (SCF) communication indispensable under sparse connectivity.
  However, intermittent contacts, finite buffers, and limited message time-to-live (TTL) often give rise to sparse delivery and congestion, leading to substantial end-to-end performance degradation.
  To address this challenge, this study explores the joint optimization of decentralized opportunistic routing and controllable unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) flight, aiming to enlarge future contacts through discrete UAV headings while enabling per-node replication under contact-limited observations.
  Building upon this architecture, we study cooperative factored routing--UAV control under centralized training and decentralized execution (CTDE) and propose JUROR (Joint UAV flight and Opportunistic Routing, based on the proximal policy optimization (PPO) framework.
  In our design, we first cast the problem as a factored partially observable Markov decision process with sequential motion--routing coupling and a per-step team reward; subsequently, decentralized actors act on local observations while a training-time critic uses global statistics, and an optional multi-horizon hotspot predictor provides auxiliary supervision.
  Simulation results over four traffic modes demonstrate effective gains over PRoPHET and MaxProp, while retaining contact-limited decentralized execution.

</details>


### [130] [Agreement Before Diversity: Verification-First Complementarity for Heterogeneous Language-Model Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04618)
*Ruitong Li,Binjie Guo,Aisheng Mo,Guowei Su,Jie Li,Ru Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Agreement-Before-Diversity (ABD)方法，将异构语言模型集成中的"替换权"与"候选多样性"解耦，通过冻结的、无标签的决策规则——锚定答案在获得两个额外可信样本的一致印证时被保留，否则由异构合成答案替换——实现可审计的答案择优机制。作者证明了两个精确恒等式，将准确率差距分解为可枚举的保护层贡献，并在LiveCodeBench-v6和GPQA-Diamond上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构语言模型集成扩大了候选答案空间，但缺乏一个原则性的判据来确定新生成的答案何时应取代已被支持的答案。现有方法未能区分"候选多样性空间的大小"（headroom）与"替换权威性"（authority），导致择优过程不透明、不可审计。

Method: ABD是一种冻结的、无需标签的决策规则：锚定答案保留当且仅当两个额外的可信采样样本在固定等价关系下佐证它；否则由异构合成答案替换。作者证明了两个精确恒等式，分别刻画相对无条件合成和相对永不合成的准确率差距，两个恒等式均不假设独立性或校准置信度。期望推理成本约为8-5×覆盖率（调用次数）。

Result: 在盲法、精确ID评估下，ABD在完整LiveCodeBench-v6上达59.43%（对比Single9的52.57%和HAC的52.00%，n=175），在未经接触的GPQA-Diamond分割上达75.00%（两个对照组均为72.78%，n=180）。恒等式将全部聚合差异定位到可枚举的保护层：LiveCodeBench上3个保护案例中无不一致项（覆盖率将门的贡献先验限制在1.71点）；GPQA-Diamond的132例中13对8个不一致案例；冻结锚定扰动下71例中12对0。

Conclusion: 多样化提供候选潜力，而验证结构提供替换权威。通过将替换权重构为显式、可审计的对象并辅以精确的分解恒等式，ABD在提升异构集成准确率的同时，实现了择优决策的完全可解释性和可枚举的可审计性。
