Abstract: Heterogeneous language-model ensembles expand the space of candidate responses, yet they lack a principled criterion for when a newly generated answer should supersede an already supported one. We decouple candidate headroom from replacement authority, rendering the latter as an explicit, auditable object. Our proposed method, Agreement-Before-Diversity (ABD), is a frozen, label-free decision rule: an anchor answer is retained if two additional trusted samples corroborate it under a fixed equivalence relation; otherwise, it is replaced by a heterogeneous synthesis. For this gating mechanism, we prove two exact identities. The first shows that the accuracy gap relative to unconditional synthesis is determined jointly by the agreement coverage and the anchor's advantage on the protected subset. The second shows that the gap relative to never synthesizing reflects a contrast between authorized recovery and authorized destruction. Neither identity assumes independence or calibrated confidence, and the expected inference cost is approximately eight minus five times the coverage in number of calls. Under blind, exact-ID evaluation, ABD achieves 59.43% on the complete LiveCodeBench-v6 (vs. 52.57% for Single9 and 52.00% for HAC; n = 175) and 75.00% on an untouched GPQA-Diamond split (both controls at 72.78%; n = 180). Furthermore, these identities localize every aggregate difference to an enumerable protected stratum: no discordant items occur among the 3 protected cases on LiveCodeBench, where coverage bounds the gate's contribution to 1.71 points a priori; 13 versus 8 discordant cases among 132 on GPQA-Diamond; and 12 versus 0 among 71 under a frozen anchor perturbation. Diversity supplies potential; verification structure supplies authority.

</details>


### [131] [A/B Agent: A Self-Evolving Agent for Strategy Iteration in Industrial A/B Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04625)
*Zhuohang Jiang,Yuxin Chen,Yongsen Pan,Zheng Hu,Wenqi Fan,Qing Li,Hongyang Wang,Jun Wang,Wenwu Ou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出A/B Agent，一个用于工业推荐系统策略优化的闭环A/B智能体框架。它通过将历史策略组织成层次化经验树、利用多路径Tree-RAG检索可迁移证据生成策略，并基于在线A/B反馈持续自进化，解决了传统人工调参效率低下和历史实验知识难以系统复用的问题。在真实短视频电商推荐系统中实现了4.829%的GMV提升，且所有护栏指标均保持正向增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业推荐系统策略迭代严重依赖大规模A/B实验，传统方法需要专家反复设计策略、配置实验和分析结果，劳动密集且耗时。同时，历史实验知识碎片化，难以通过人工实现系统性复用。现有RAG智能体以扁平方式组织经验，忽略了业务场景、推荐阶段、优化目标和实验语境之间的层次关系，导致检索不匹配、跨场景迁移受限，且无法通过连续A/B反馈持续优化策略。

Method: 提出A/B Agent框架，包含三个紧密耦合的核心组件：1) 历史策略知识组织——将历史策略构建为层次化经验树；2) 自主目标感知策略生成——通过多路径Tree-RAG检索可迁移证据，生成可执行的推荐策略；3) 实验引导的策略自进化——持续分析在线A/B反馈，自主调参并更新经验树，形成闭环的自进化机制。

Result: 通过大量离线和在线评估验证了框架的有效性。在真实的短视频电商推荐系统中，GMV提升了4.829%，且所有护栏指标均保持正向增益。

Conclusion: A/B Agent通过层次化经验组织、目标感知检索生成和实验反馈驱动的自进化，实现了工业推荐策略优化的闭环自动化，有效解决了传统人工调参与现有RAG方法在知识组织层级和跨场景迁移方面的局限。

Abstract: Industrial recommendation strategy iteration heavily relies on large-scale A/B experimentation. Traditional tuning requires experts to repeatedly design strategies, configure experiments, analyze results, and adjust parameters, making the process labor-intensive and time-consuming. Meanwhile, valuable knowledge from historical experiments is often fragmented, making systematic reuse difficult through manual expert effort alone. Existing RAG agents partially alleviate this burden by retrieving prior strategies, but typically organize experience in a flat manner, overlooking the hierarchical relationships among business scenarios, recommendation stages, optimization objectives, and experimental contexts. This often results in mismatched retrieval and limited cross-scenario transfer, while preventing agents from continuously refining strategies and parameters through sequential A/B feedback. %
To address these limitations, we propose A/B Agent, a closed-loop A/B agent for industrial recommendation strategy optimization. The framework comprises three tightly coupled core components: Historical Strategy Knowledge Organization, Autonomous Target-Aware Strategy Generation, and Experiment-Guided Strategy Self-Evolution. It organizes historical strategies into a hierarchical experience tree, retrieves transferable evidence through multi-path Tree-RAG to generate executable strategies, and continuously analyzes online A/B feedback to guide autonomous tuning and update the experience tree for self-evolution. Extensive offline and online evaluations demonstrate its effectiveness, including a 4.829% improvement in GMV in a real-world short-video e-commerce recommendation system while maintaining positive gains across all guardrail metrics.

</details>


### [132] [AI Literacy for Legal Translation: Developing Digital Resilience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04641)
*Łucja Biel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨生成式AI对法律翻译专业的影响，提出了一个包含基础性、程序性、批判性和战略性四个相互强化维度的AI素养框架，并将其概念化为法律翻译能力的横向组成部分，以促进数字韧性。文章还讨论了该框架的教学意义，提出了培养法律翻译学生AI素养的课堂活动设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI在带来机遇的同时，也给法律翻译引入了语言、技术、法律、伦理和认知等多方面的风险。法律翻译专业人员需要系统性地应对这些挑战，但目前缺乏针对该职业量身定制的AI素养框架，因此有必要探讨AI对专业法律翻译的影响并提出相应的能力框架。

Method: 本文采用概念性论述方法，通过分析生成式AI对法律翻译的多维影响，提出一个由基础性、程序性、批判性和战略性四个相互强化的维度构成的AI素养框架，将AI素养概念化为法律翻译能力的横向组成部分，并进一步探讨该框架在翻译教育中的教学应用。

Result: 提出了一套适用于法律翻译职业的四维AI素养框架，论证了AI并未改变法律翻译的根本目标，但要求通过AI素养扩展专业能力；同时设计了基于该框架的课堂活动，旨在帮助未来译者批判性、负责任地并按照专业标准整合AI工具。

Conclusion: AI素养应被视为法律翻译能力中不可或缺的横向要素，法律翻译教育需要系统性地纳入AI素养培养，以使未来译者具备数字韧性和专业竞争力，在AI辅助环境中坚守法律翻译的专业标准与伦理规范。

Abstract: Generative AI is transforming legal translation by introducing opportunities alongside linguistic, technical, legal, ethical and cognitive risks. This chapter examines the implications of AI for professional legal translation and proposes an AI literacy framework tailored to the profession. It argues that AI does not change the fundamental objectives of legal translation but requires an extension of professional competence through AI literacy. The proposed framework comprises four mutually reinforcing dimensions, foundational, procedural, critical and strategic, and conceptualises AI literacy as a transversal component of legal translation competence that fosters digital resilience. It further discusses the pedagogical implications of this framework by proposing classroom activities designed to develop AI literacy in legal translator education, enabling future translators to integrate AI critically, responsibly and in accordance with professional standards.

</details>


### [133] [Calibrating Artificial Guilt: Neurally Grounded Reward Shaping for Prosocial Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04663)
*Aaditya Mehta,Arya Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文利用人类fMRI数据（SoDec数据集，40名参与者）拟合回归模型，从幸福感变化中校准出"愧疚权重"（w=1.118），并将其嵌入多智能体强化学习的奖励塑形中。在双智能体Social Lottery环境中，采用神经校准的PPO智能体行为与人类社会安全选择率最接近（0.459 vs 0.484，KL=0.0012），显著优于其他三种塑形方案，表明人类神经行为先验可作为亲社会奖励塑形的定量约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体强化学习中通常会在个体奖励中加入社会项来促进合作，但这些社会项的权重通常依赖手工设定，缺乏系统性标定依据。作者希望探究能否从人类神经与行为数据中校准"愧疚"信号（guilt signal），并迁移到人工智能体中，从而替代人为试错的超参数选择。

Method: 使用公开的SoDec责任fMRI数据集（40名参与者），构建受试者固定效应回归模型，将"当下幸福感变化"（momentary happiness changes）对结果类型计数进行回归，以"Partner-negative减去Social-negative"的对比来恢复愧疚权重（w=1.118，Cohen's d=0.214）。将该权重嵌入双智能体Social Lottery环境，训练独立PPO actor-critic智能体，比较四种奖励塑形方案：神经校准的、均匀常数、零（自私）以及单位系数的oracle方案。每种条件下进行1,000次评估回合。

Result: 神经校准的智能体在所有评估条件下与人类Social safe-choice率最为接近（0.459 vs 人工0.484，KL散度=0.0012），而其他三种条件（均匀常数、零自私、单位系数oracle）的KL散度偏差高出1-3个数量级。

Conclusion: 人类神经行为先验可以作为奖励塑形中亲社会项的定量约束，校准出的愧疚权重能有效转移至人工智能体，使智能体行为更贴近人类真实社会决策模式，为多智能体系统中社会奖励权重的确定提供了一种数据驱动的替代方案。

Abstract: Cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning often adds social terms to individual rewards, yet the scale of those terms is usually chosen by hand. We ask whether a guilt signal can instead be calibrated from human neural and behavioural data and transferred to artificial agents. Using the public SoDec responsibility fMRI dataset (40 participants), we fit a subject-fixed-effects regression of momentary-happiness changes on outcome-type counts and recover a guilt weight as the Partner-negative minus Social-negative contrast ($\hat{w}=1.118$, Cohen's $d=0.214$). We embed this weight in a two-agent Social Lottery environment and train independent Proximal Policy Optimization actor-critics under four shaping regimes: neurally calibrated, uniform constant, zero (selfish), and a unit-coefficient oracle. Across 1{,}000 evaluation episodes per condition, the calibrated agents track the human Social safe-choice rate most closely ($0.459$ vs.\ human $0.484$; $\mathrm{KL}=0.0012$), while the other three conditions deviate by one to three orders of magnitude in KL. Human neurobehavioural priors can therefore act as quantitative constraints on prosocial reward shaping.

</details>


### [134] [Traceable LLM-Generated Hazard Scenarios for Operational Safety Analysis of Aviation Systems Using ASRS Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04697)
*Cristian Mascia,Roberto Pietrantuono,Daniel Rodriguez,Stefano Russo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种AI辅助方法，从NASA航空安全报告系统（ASRS）中生成候选危险情景，用于航线运行危险分析。该方法针对目标不利后果，生成包含分类因素的结构化假设和叙事性情景描述，并附带基于历史共现证据的可信度评分及与相似ASRS报告的溯源。进一步提出混合变体，通过进化溯因生成结构化假设来条件化叙事生成，提升正确性并降低变异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空系统运行的危险分析必须考虑天气、ATC行动、空域约束、航空器运行和人为因素之间的交互，这与飞机系统层面的功能危险评估（FHA）不同。现有方法缺乏系统性地从历史数据中生成候选危险情景的手段，需要一种能够自动化、可追溯且带可信度评估的情景生成方法。

Method: 利用NASA的ASRS数据库，采用大语言模型生成候选危险情景。给定目标不利后果，模型输出两类结果：(1)结构化假设（分类因素）和(2)叙事性情景（描述与结构一致的运行事件序列）。每个情景附带基于历史共现证据的可信度评分，并提供与最相似保留ASRS报告的溯源。同时提出混合变体，使用进化溯因算法先生成结构化假设，再以此条件化叙事生成。评估涉及多个大语言模型、零样本与少样本提示以及可选微调。

Result: 通过系统性评估多个大语言模型、不同的提示策略（零样本vs少样本）以及微调选项，衡量了这些因素对生成结构与叙事有效性和真实性的影响。进化溯因驱动的混合变体在提高生成正确性和降低输出变异性方面表现更优。

Conclusion: 所提出的AI辅助危险情景生成方法能够为航线运行危险分析提供结构化、可追溯且带可信度评估的候选情景，其中结合进化溯因的混合方案在生成的正确性和一致性方面具有显著优势。

Abstract: Operational hazard analysis of aviation system operations must consider interactions among weather, ATC actions, airspace constraints, aircraft operations, and human factors - distinct from the functional hazard assessment applied at the aircraft-system level. We present an AI-assisted approach that generates candidate hazard scenarios from NASA's Aviation Safety Reporting System (ASRS). Given a target adverse outcome, it produces a structured hypothesis as categorical factors and a narrative scenario describing an operational event sequence consistent with the structure. Each scenario includes by a plausibility score from historical co-occurrence evidence and traceability to the most similar held-out ASRS reports. We then propose a hybrid variant, conditioning narrative generation on a structured hypothesis produced via evolutionary abduction, improving correctness and reducing variability. We evaluate multiple large language models, zero-shot versus few-shot prompting, and optional fine-tuning, measuring how prompting and model choice affect the validity and realism of the generated structures and narratives.

</details>


### [135] [Diagnosing Tool-Selection Reasoning in LLM Agents with Canary Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04719)
*Atul Anand,Sourav Chattaraj*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"金丝雀工具"（canary tools）诊断框架，通过在智能体MCP工具集内植入六类探针工具，将模型单一的"选错工具"信号转化为多维度的工具推理弱点画像。对8个模型、120个任务、8,640次运行的系统评估揭示了模型能力与工具选择易感性之间的复杂关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的智能体评估只能告诉我们模型选错了工具，却无法解释为什么选错。作者希望将模糊的"错误"转化为可解析、可诊断的多维弱点画像，为模型工具推理能力的改进提供细粒度依据。

Method: 设计六类金丝雀工具探针（语义诱饵、参数陷阱、能力幻象、先决条件盲区、时序诱饵、粒度陷阱），植入智能体的MCP工具集。评估8个模型（6个托管模型和2个8B开源模型），在120个任务、3种金丝雀密度条件和3个随机种子下进行8,640次运行，并附加2,880次运行的微妙消融实验。效果由提供方无关的裁判模型评分，并由第二位独立裁判交叉验证（Cohen's kappa = 0.75）。

Result: 三个核心发现：（1）模型越强则易感性越低，各模型每任务金丝雀易感率（CSR）跨度约36倍，Claude Opus 4.8最低、Llama 3.1 8B最高；（2）能力层级不能单独预测安全性——最易感的中端托管模型，且同一供应商内更便宜的模型反而更安全；（3）分类法与能力分层对应：能力幻象最易困住前沿模型，其余类型对强模型基本无效但对小模型有效，因此这些探针按能力分化而非单纯"弱"。软化金丝雀的提示性措辞后前沿模型CSR基本不变，证明探针衡量的是推理而非措辞匹配。易感性与任务失败相关（Spearman rho = -0.34），最稳健的模型不受金丝雀压力显著影响。

Conclusion: 金丝雀工具框架提供了将工具选择错误转化为可解析诊断画像的有效手段，并揭示了模型能力、成本与工具推理安全性之间的非线性关系，对智能体工具安全评估具有重要价值。框架、金丝雀模式、任务与日志已开源发布。

Abstract: Agent evaluations tell us that a model picked the wrong tool, but rarely why. We introduce canary tools: diagnostic probe tools planted in an agent's Model Context Protocol (MCP) tool set, each engineered to probe one specific tool-selection weakness. A six-type taxonomy (semantic decoys, parameter traps, capability mirages, prerequisite blindness, temporal decoys, and granularity traps) turns a single "wrong tool" outcome into a multi-dimensional profile of how a model reasons about tools. We evaluate eight models -- six hosted and two 8B open-weight -- spanning three capability tiers, on 120 tasks across three canary-density conditions and three seeds (8,640 runs), plus a 2,880-run subtlety ablation. Task success is graded by a provider-independent judge, corroborated by a second independent judge (Cohen's kappa = 0.75). We report three findings. First, susceptibility drops sharply as models get more capable: the per-task canary susceptibility rate (CSR) ranges about 36x across models, lowest for Claude Opus 4.8 and highest for Llama 3.1 8B. Second, capability tier alone does not predict safety: the most susceptible hosted model is mid-tier, and within a provider the cheaper model can be the safer one. Third, the taxonomy is capability-stratified: capability mirages most reliably trap frontier models, while the other types are largely inert on strong models but fire on small open models, so they discriminate by capability rather than being weak. Softening each canary's give-away phrase leaves frontier CSR essentially unchanged, evidence that the probes measure reasoning, not phrase-spotting. Susceptibility also predicts task failure (Spearman rho = -0.34), while the most robust models are not significantly degraded by canary pressure. We release the framework, canary schemas, tasks, and logs.

</details>


### [136] [When Prompts Become Pixels: Prompt-Region Grounding for Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04726)
*Yongxin Wang,Ruizhe Zhou,Yueling Tang,Yingying Zhu,Xuemin Zhao,Xiaojun Chang,Xiaodan Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Visualized Task Semantics (VTS)，通过将任务问题转移到图像像素中来评估多模态大语言模型的指令跟随能力。研究发现24个模型-任务组合中准确率平均下降17.8个百分点，揭示了超越OCR的"语义通道差距"。作者提出提示区域接地（prompt-region grounding）方法，在训练成本匹配的条件下将VTS准确率从58.0提升至66.3，同时保持原始界面性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准通常将问题放在文本通道中，导致模型评估忽略了"任务本身以像素形式呈现"的场景。这掩盖了一个关键问题：模型是否在图像和文本两种通道上同等有效地使用指令。研究者希望通过受控干预来系统性地揭示这种可能的通道差异。

Method: 1) VTS干预：将问题移入图像中，保持源问题和答案不变。2) 跨6个MLLM和4个基准进行系统评估。3) 提出提示区域接地方法：将问题区域与类型化语义对齐，从遮挡视图中恢复干净的表示，训练成本与基线匹配，推理时无需OCR或区域元数据。

Result: 所有24个模型-任务组合准确率均下降，平均下降17.8个百分点。模型常能正确转录视觉问题但无法使用它。VTS方法将四基准VTS准确率从58.0提升至66.3，同时保持原始界面性能。

Conclusion: 读取任务承载文本与将其作为推理指令进行接地是两个截然不同的能力。提示区域接地可以有效弥合语义通道差距，揭示出当前MLLM在跨通道指令跟随上的系统性弱点。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models increasingly reason over screenshots and documents where the task itself may be written in pixels. Yet benchmarks usually place questions in text, leaving it unclear whether models use the same instruction equally well across channels. We introduce Visualized Task Semantics (VTS), a controlled intervention that moves the question into the image while keeping the source problem and answer fixed. Across six MLLMs and four benchmarks, accuracy drops in all 24 model-task pairs, by 17.8 points on average. Models often transcribe the visual question correctly yet fail to use it, exposing a semantic channel gap beyond OCR. To reduce this gap, we present prompt-region grounding, whose core design aligns the question region with typed semantics and recovers its clean representation from a masked view. At matched training cost, our method raises four-benchmark VTS accuracy from 58.0 to 66.3 while preserving accuracy on the original interface, and requires no OCR or region metadata at inference. Reading task-bearing text and grounding it as an instruction for reasoning are distinct capabilities.

</details>


### [137] [Chain-of-Thought Monitoring Can Be Unreliable in Implicit-Influence Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04735)
*Agatha Duzan,Asa Cooper Stickland*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次构建了对比隐式和显式影响下CoT可监控性的基准，发现显式设置会高估模型思维链的可监控性，且开发者的良性系统提示会进一步降低隐式场景下的检测率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的CoT监控评估主要集中在显式影响设置（提示直接指示模型隐藏信息），但忽视了隐式影响设置（提示无隐藏指令，但任务特征或上下文仍会塑造模型行为）。理解两种场景下监控能力的差异，对于评估前沿推理模型的安全层实际效果至关重要。

Method: 构建首个直接比较两种影响机制下CoT可监控性的基准。使用"轻推"（nudge）促使模型选择特定选项，通过两种方式传递：隐性轻推（随意的旁白）和显性指令（直接指示模型执行并隐藏）。基准覆盖四种任务格式（二选一、数值评分、多项选择问答、开放式编码）和七个前沿扩展思考模型。

Result: 显式影响下CoT监控器能检测60-94%的行为偏移（即使被指示隐藏也会泄露指令）；隐式影响下检测率在四个设置的两种中下降41-46个百分点。真实的系统提示添加（如开发者部署以减少离题偏差）将隐式检测率进一步降至低至5%，同时保留行为影响。

Conclusion: 显式影响设置下的监控评估可能高估模型的可监控性，且出于良好意图的部署选择（如系统提示）会进一步削弱监控能力，导致实际部署中CoT监控层的可靠性低于当前预期。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring is increasingly treated as an important safety layer for frontier reasoning models. Most monitorability evaluations study explicit-influence settings: setups where the prompt directly incentivizes the model to hide something, e.g., by instructing it to perform a hidden side-task. A complementary axis for CoT-monitor evaluations is implicit-influence settings, where the prompt contains no instruction to hide, but the model's behavior is still shaped by features of the task or context, e.g. an irrelevant detail about a candidate that biases a hiring rating.
  We introduce the first benchmark that directly compares CoT monitorability under the two regimes. We test how model behavior changes in the presence of a nudge to choose a particular option. The nudge is delivered either as a casual aside (implicit), or as a direct instruction to act on the nudge and to conceal having done so (explicit). The benchmark spans four task formats (binary choice, numeric rating, multiple-choice QA, open-ended coding) and seven frontier extended-thinking models.
  Under explicit influence, a CoT monitor detects 60-94% of behavior shifts: even models instructed to conceal it leak the instruction into their CoT. Under implicit influence, the same factors still shift behavior, but detection falls by 41-46 percentage points in two of our four settings. Realistic system-prompt additions (of the kind a developer might deploy to reduce off-topic bias) lower implicit detection further, to as low as 5%, while preserving the behavioral influence itself.
  These results suggest that monitorability estimates obtained in explicit-influence settings may over-estimate monitorability, and that monitorability can be further decreased by well-intentioned deployment choices. Our benchmark and code are available at https://github.com/agatha-duzan/implicit-vs-explicit-influence

</details>


### [138] [EviGraph: Evidence-Guided Autonomous Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04738)
*Zhenjiang Ren,Ruiji Li,Xujing Zhang,Ziliang Pang,Shuo Ren,Jiajun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EviGraph是一个自主研究框架，将研究过程表示为一个带类型标注的证据图（包含Problem、Gap、Hypothesis、Experiment、Finding和Claim节点），并以此作为代理的运行状态。该框架通过检查证据链的缺失依赖、语义错位和结果声明不一致性，定位最早的薄弱节点并重新生成受影响的子图，通过图检查点防止修复失败破坏已验证的证据，仅在每个保留声明都有充分证据链支持时才生成手稿。在ARC-Bench-ML和NanoResearch-20基准上，EviGraph在整体研究性能上优于端到端研究代理基线，声明支持率提升40.19%，实验数据一致性达87.73%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主研究代理生成的输出常包含无支撑的声明以及与实验、结果和结论不一致的内容。作者认为这一问题部分源于架构设计：现有系统将研究组织为顺序流水线，未在各阶段间显式维护或验证不断演化的声明-证据结构。

Method: EviGraph将研究过程构建为一个带类型标注的证据图，包含Problem、Gap、Hypothesis、Experiment、Finding和Claim六类节点。图作为代理的"运行状态"而非事后记录。框架执行三个关键操作：1) 检查证据链是否存在缺失依赖、语义错位和结果-声明不一致；2) 定位最早的薄弱节点并再生其受影响的后续子图；3) 通过图检查点机制防止无效修复破坏已验证的证据。手稿仅在每个保留声明都有完整证据链支持时生成。

Result: 在ARC-Bench-ML和NanoResearch-20两个基准上的实验表明，EviGraph在整体研究性能上优于对比的端到端研究代理基线。具体而言，声明支持率相比最强基线提升40.19%，实验数据一致性达到87.73%。

Conclusion: 显式的证据状态维护对可靠的自主研究具有重要价值，EviGraph通过将研究过程建模为可验证的证据图，有效解决了自主研究代理输出中声明缺乏支撑和结果不一致的关键问题。

Abstract: Autonomous research agents can generate hypotheses, execute experiments, and draft manuscripts, yet their outputs often contain unsupported claims and inconsistencies between research questions, experiments, results, and conclusions. We argue that this problem is partly architectural: existing systems organize research as sequential pipelines but do not explicitly maintain or validate the evolving claim-evidence structure across stages.In this paper, we introduce EviGraph, an autonomous research framework that represents the research process as a typed evidence graph containing Problem, Gap, Hypothesis, Experiment, Finding, and Claim nodes. The graph serves as the operational state of the agent rather than a post-hoc record. EviGraph inspects evidence chains for missing dependencies, semantic misalignment, and result-claim inconsistencies, localizes the earliest weak node, and regenerates its affected downstream subgraph. Graph checkpointing prevents unsuccessful repairs from corrupting previously validated evidence. Manuscripts are generated only after every retained claim is grounded in a validated evidence chain.Experiments on ARC-Bench-ML and NanoResearch-20 show that EviGraph outperforms the compared end-to-end research-agent baselines in overall research performance, improves Claim Support Rate by 40.19% over the strongest baseline, and achieves 87.73% Experimental Data Consistency. These results demonstrate the value of explicit evidence-state maintenance for reliable autonomous research.

</details>


### [139] [Fewer Tokens, Smaller Cache: Reward-Coordinated Efficient Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04771)
*Qiyuan Zhu,Dezhi Li,Pengyu Cheng,Tianle Chen,Jiacheng Wang,Ruijie Shen,Hao Gu,Sida Lin,Zirui Liu,Jiacheng Liu,Sirui Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ReCo框架，利用过程奖励（process reward）协调KV-cache压缩、反射令牌惩罚和早期停止三方面，在多个推理模型和基准上减少37%-65%生成令牌和2.08x-2.35x端到端延迟，同时基本保持准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型（LRMs）通过长思维链推理在复杂任务上表现出色，但冗长的中间步骤导致严重过度思考（overthinking），增加推理成本。现有面向推理的KV-cache压缩方法在整个轨迹上施加统一策略，且仅根据移除内容来评判压缩效果。然而两点观察表明：推理状态对上下文丢失的容忍度沿轨迹变化，且过程奖励可追踪这种变化；压缩在生成端并非免费，因为更小的缓存会导致模型生成更多令牌，部分抵消节省。

Method: 提出ReCo（Reward-Coordinated Compression）步骤级框架，由轻量级过程奖励估计器对每个完成的步骤打分，驱动三个组件：(1) 奖励自适应KV-cache压缩，在高奖励步骤更激进地缩减保留缓存，在低奖励步骤压缩更少；(2) 奖励分带惩罚作用于反射令牌，抑制冗余生成；(3) 基于置信度的早期停止，在推理可靠时触发。

Result: 在三个推理模型和六个基准上，ReCo相比完整CoT减少37%-65%的生成令牌，端到端延迟降低2.08x-2.35x，同时基本保持了准确率。

Conclusion: 通过将KV-cache压缩和生成端的令牌节约统一在单一过程奖励信号下进行协调优化，ReCo有效缓解了过度思考问题，实现了推理效率和准确率之间更好的权衡。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) excel on complex tasks through long chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning, but their lengthy intermediate steps cause severe overthinking that inflates inference cost. KV-cache compression is a common solution, yet existing reasoning-oriented methods apply a uniform policy across the trajectory and judge compression only by what it removes from the cache. Two observations point the other way. First, a reasoning state's tolerance to context loss varies along the trajectory, and process reward tracks it: deleting tokens at high-reward steps preserves accuracy far better than deleting the same budget at random. Second, compression is not free on the generation side, since a smaller cache leads the model to generate more tokens, partly canceling the saving. Together these motivate coordinating both sides under a single process reward. We propose ReCo (Reward-Coordinated Compression), a step-wise framework in which a lightweight process-reward estimator scores each completed step and drives three components: (1) reward-adaptive KV-cache compression that shrinks the retained cache harder at high-reward steps and less at low-reward ones, (2) a reward-banded penalty on reflection tokens that curbs redundant generation, and (3) confidence-based early stopping that triggers when the reasoning is reliable. Across three reasoning models and six benchmarks, ReCo reduces generated tokens by 37%-65% and end-to-end latency by 2.08x-2.35x over Full CoT, all while largely preserving accuracy.

</details>


### [140] [NSF-HRPT: Neural Semantic Field meets Hierarchical Risk Perception Tree for Safety-Critical Scenario Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04776)
*Yu Zhao,Jiangyu Pan,Tao Hu,Ming Yin,Fan Yang,Jiangfan Liu,Xiubo Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出NSF-HRPT框架，结合神经语义场（NSF）与层次化风险感知树（HRPT），实现从单目视觉输入的定量风险评估，在合成与真实数据集上达到最优或接近最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有碰撞预测研究虽有进展，但面临两大挑战：多智能体交互的复杂动态使得从单目输入精确量化风险水平困难；真实环境存在固有不确定性。需要一个既能高效建模场景语义又能量化风险的框架。

Method: 1) 神经语义场（NSF）：从仿真数据中学习场景语义、轨迹预测和概率化TTC分布，作为推理先验。2) 层次化风险感知树（HRPT）：利用预训练NSF作为先验，实现并行计算和多智能体风险的空间推理。3) Sim2Real增强策略：通过引入基础模型先验提升真实世界适用性，无需重新训练。

Result: 在合成基准上达到最先进（SOTA）性能；在真实世界数据集上实现接近SOTA的竞争性结果，涵盖TTC估计精度和风险定位精度两个指标。

Conclusion: NSF-HRPT为单目相机输入提供了实时风险感知的有效解决方案，验证了学习感知与结构化推理结合在安全关键场景中的可行性。

Abstract: The ability to accurately assess and anticipate risks in safety-critical scenarios is crucial for autonomous driving systems. While existing research has made progress in collision prediction, accurately quantifying risk levels from monocular vision inputs remains challenging due to the complex dynamics of multi-agent interactions and the inherent uncertainty in real-world environments. To address these challenges, we present NSF-HRPT, a novel framework that combines learning-based perception with structured reasoning for quantitative risk assessment. Our approach features a Neural Semantic Field (NSF) that learns to model scene semantics, trajectory predictions, and probabilistic Time-to-Collision (TTC) distributions from simulation data. During inference, the pre-trained NSF serves as a prior for our Hierarchical Risk Perception Tree (HRPT), which enables efficient parallel computation and spatial reasoning about multi-agent risks. Additionally, we introduce a Sim2Real enhancement strategy that improves real-world applicability without retraining by incorporating priors from foundation models. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that our framework achieves state-of-the-art performance on synthetic benchmarks and delivers competitive, near-state-of-the-art results on real-world datasets for both TTC estimation accuracy and risk localization precision. The proposed method provides an effective solution for real-time risk awareness from monocular camera inputs.

</details>


### [141] [Privileged, but Biased: How PI-Conditioned Teachers Break Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04794)
*Sarthak Harne,Chinmay Karkar,Yash Pandya,Ahmed Awadallah,Akshay Nambi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统性地揭示了自我蒸馏（SD）作为唯一目标函数时的失效机制。虽然在简单任务中SD能复现SDPO报告的性能提升，但在困难任务（问答、数学、代码、多轮Agent工具使用）中，单个token损失持续下降但验证准确率无法改善甚至退化。作者通过一条因果链解释了这一失效：教师受特权信息（PI）偏差影响，其逐token目标偏向特定参考轨迹而非普遍正确性；学生匹配该目标导致目标函数与正确性解耦，损失集中在停用词、标点等低信息token上，反而惩罚了推理所需的探索性token，最终产生更平坦、更不果断且推理能力无提升的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我蒸馏（SD）被提出作为强化学习（RLVR）的计算高效替代方案，已有报告显示其性能提升，但这些提升几乎全部来自狭窄、低难度设定。核心悬而未决的问题是：作为唯一的优化目标（不含奖励项），SD是否真正教会了学生模型任何东西？作者旨在超越简单的成功/失败二分法，在困难任务上检验SD的有效性，并探究其失效的根本原因。

Method: 作者首先在SDPO的简单设定中复现其报告的性能提升，然后将完全相同的实验设置应用于困难任务（包括问答、数学、编码、多轮agent工具使用），涵盖多种推理模式、模型规模和特权信息形式，并同时测试SDPO和OPSD两种配方。为解释失效原因，作者提出PI偏差分数（PI Bias Score）来量化教师因看到特定参考解而产生的逐token目标偏差，并通过损失分配分析（区分低信息token与高信息token、正确与非正确rollout）来追踪因果链。

Result: 实验结果显示：在困难任务上，逐token损失持续稳定下降，但验证准确率没有提升且通常发生退化。该结果在问答、数学、编码、多轮agent工具使用等所有任务类型、所有模型规模、推理模式及PI形式下均一致。因果链验证包括：教师存在PI偏差导致目标偏离普遍正确性；学生的损失函数与rollout是否正确几乎无关；损失主要集中在停用词、标点、不确定性标记等低信息token；在正确的rollout内部，探索性token承受最高散度，即SD惩罚了推理所需的犹豫/探索行为。

Conclusion: 自我蒸馏作为一个孤立目标函数，优化的是一种与任务成功解耦的信号——它压缩的是低信息token的分布而非推理关键token的正确性。这使得SD在易设定中看似有效（学生只需模仿教师），但在困难任务中不仅无益反而有害。作者建议，SD不应在无奖励项的情况下作为唯一目标使用，其应用需要重新审视或结合可验证奖励信号。

Abstract: Self-distillation (SD) has emerged as a compute-efficient alternative to reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards: a self-teacher, conditioned on privileged information (PI) about the answer such as a reference solution, supplies dense per-token supervision to a student that never sees it. Reported gains, however, come almost exclusively from narrow, low-difficulty settings, leaving open a basic question: as a lone objective, with no reward term, does SD teach anything? We reproduce SDPO's reported gains in its easy setting, then apply the identical setup to difficult tasks and find that it does not. Across question answering, mathematics, coding, and multi-turn agentic tool use, across reasoning modes, model sizes, and forms of PI, and under both the SDPO and OPSD recipes, the per-token loss falls steadily while validation accuracy does not improve and typically degrades. We explain this failure through a single causal chain from the loss to the model it produces. The chain begins with PI bias: having seen one particular reference solution, the teacher's per-token target is pulled toward that trajectory rather than toward correctness in general, an effect we quantify with a PI Bias Score. Trained to match this target everywhere, the student's objective becomes nearly blind to whether a rollout is correct, and the loss it assigns falls mostly on low-information tokens like stopwords, punctuation, uncertainty markers, rather than those that determine the answer; within correct rollouts the exploratory tokens incur the highest divergence, so it penalizes the hesitation that reasoning requires. The result is a flatter, less decisive student that is no better at reasoning: as a lone objective, SD optimizes a signal decoupled from task success.

</details>


### [142] [ContextWeave: A Real-World Workflow Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04830)
*Bo Wang,Yuqian Yao,Enxi Wang,Luozhijie Jin,Yang Liu,Yiran Suo,Yuxuan Cai,Enyu Zhou,Yufei Gao,Honglin Guo,Tianyu Huai,Li Ji,Zhikai Lei,Bufan Li,Lizhi Lin,Jinxiu Liu,Jie Yang,Jiazheng Zhou,Maosen Zhou,Pengfang Qian,Shichun Liu,Guanshan Liu,Hao Zheng,Yunhao Yu,Hang Yan,Jihua Kang,Xinchi Chen,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ContextWeave，一个面向长期办公工作流的纵向评测基准，评估记忆（回忆）是否真正提升语言智能体在下游任务中的表现。通过14名参与者多月的隐私保护工作流重构出1,005个可执行任务（含568个核心评测任务），在固定模型下，最强记忆配置将Workspace Score从68.08提升至78.20，Preference Score从41.50提升至70.60；在固定记忆组件下，回忆可改善所有5种基础模型的表现。实验还发现，可执行的、经验丰富的记忆比紧凑摘要更有利于工作流延续并减少冗余探索，但更容易受误导性回忆影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对语言智能体记忆能力的评测往往将其简化为检索或问答任务，忽略了记忆是否真正改善下游智能体在长期、有状态工作流中的实际表现这一核心问题。

Method: 构建ContextWeave纵向基准：将14名参与者的隐私保护多月工作流重构为1,005个可执行任务（568个核心评测任务），配备指令、容器化环境、轨迹和任务专属评分标准；衡量工作区质量与参与者个性化偏好对齐度，并辅以相关性、连续性、可解性及对误导性回忆的鲁棒性诊断。在6种记忆组件（固定模型）和5种基础模型（固定记忆组件）下进行系统性评测。

Result: 固定模型下，最强记忆配置使Workspace Score从68.08提升到78.20，Preference Score从41.50提升到70.60；固定记忆组件下，回忆对全部5种基础模型均带来收益但幅度差异显著。可执行的、富含经验的记忆优于紧凑摘要，但更易受误导性回忆的负面影响。

Conclusion: 记忆系统不应只优化检索相关性，还需保障记忆在执行过程中的可靠使用；这一发现为设计面向长期工作流的记忆系统提供了指引。

Abstract: Memory is essential as language agents move from isolated tasks to long-horizon, stateful workflows, yet existing evaluations often reduce it to retrieval or question answering. We introduce ContextWeave, a longitudinal benchmark that evaluates whether recalled experience improves downstream agent performance in realistic office-work streams. ContextWeave reconstructs privacy-preserved, multi-month workflows of 14 participants into 1,005 executable tasks, including 568 core evaluation tasks, with instructions, containerized environments, trajectories, and task-specific rubrics. It measures workspace quality and alignment with participant-specific preferences, complemented by diagnostics of relevance, continuity, solvability, and robustness to misleading recall. Across six memory components under a fixed model, the strongest configuration raises Workspace Score from 68.08 to 78.20 and Preference Score from 41.50 to 70.60. With a fixed memory component, recall improves both outcomes for all five tested base models, although gains vary substantially. Our analysis shows that actionable, experience-rich memory supports workflow continuation and reduces redundant exploration more effectively than compact summaries, while it can also be more susceptible to misleading recall. These findings motivate memory systems that optimize not only retrieval relevance but also reliable use during execution.

</details>


### [143] [When Shared Rollouts Fail in Defensive Driving Evaluation: A NAVSIM Score Basis Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04896)
*Ziang Wei,Minjun Yu,Zheyuan Lai,Mingjie Pang,Wei Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文审计了NAVSIM v2.2基准测试中防御性驾驶评分的数值稳定性问题，发现参考条件化宽容机制会因共享的不稳定轨迹重放变换而将参考失败传播为广泛合规信用，导致忽略所有障碍物的探针模型排名超过人类重放与闭环策略。通过同源依赖栈控制与精确输入诊断，定位到共享速度参数重拟合中的依赖性敏感数值行为，替换求解器即可消除发散并恢复合理排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 防御性驾驶评分只有在能够区分"观察周围参与者"与"不观察"的策略时才有意义。然而重模拟基准中的参考条件化宽容机制，在智能体与人类参考共享不稳定的轨迹重放变换时，可能将共享的参考失败传播为广泛的合规信用，从而破坏评分的区分能力，需要对此风险进行审计。

Method: 在NAVSIM v2.2原始场景单阶段评分上，使用路由盲的Ignore-All探针和路由感知的actor盲探针进行审计；采用固定32-token诊断集复现滚动发散；通过同源依赖栈控制和精确输入诊断隔离数值敏感行为；在450-token控制池中仅替换求解器以验证数值不稳定性是直接触发因素。

Result: 在完整12,146 token navtest划分上，路由盲的Ignore-All探针与路由感知的actor盲探针排名超过了人类重放和PDM-Closed。相同源码依赖栈控制显示数值行为源自共享速度参数重拟合；仅替换求解器即可消除滚动发散并恢复"盲排在最后"的排序，同时保持宽容机制启用。

Conclusion: 数值不稳定性是导致评分失效的直接触发因素，参考条件化宽容机制将共享参考失败传播为合规信用。因此提出审计协议，要求披露评分基线与依赖栈、引入盲探针、覆盖报告以及滚动稳定性测试，才能将此类评分用于防御性驾驶主张。

Abstract: Defensive driving scores are useful only when they preserve distinctions between policies that observe surrounding actors and those that do not. Re-simulation benchmarks may use reference-conditioned forgiveness, under which an agent receives credit when the logged human reference fails a compliance channel. When agent and reference share an unstable rollout transformation, this rule can propagate shared reference failures into broad compliance credit.
  We audit this risk in NAVSIM v2.2 original scene single-stage scoring. Under the affected documented-stack condition on the audited numerical backend, the route-blind Ignore-All probe and a route-aware actor-blind probe outrank human replay and PDM-Closed over the complete 12,146-token navtest split. A fresh installation following the public specification reproduces rollout divergence on a fixed 32-token diagnostic set. A same-source dependency stack control and an exact-input diagnostic isolate dependency-sensitive numerical behavior in the shared velocity refit. On a 450-token control pool, replacing only the solver eliminates rollout divergence and restores blind-last ordering while keeping forgiveness enabled. Thus, the numerical instability is the direct trigger. Reference-conditioned forgiveness propagates the resulting shared reference failures into compliance credit. We contribute an audit protocol requiring score basis and stack disclosure, blind probes, overwrite reporting, and rollout stability tests before using such scores for defensive driving claims.

</details>


### [144] [WorldCycle: Self-Verifiable Reinforcement Learning for Long-Horizon Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04964)
*Bohai Gu,Yueyang Yuan,Taiyi Wu,Dazhao Du,Jian Liu,Xiaoyi Pang,Jie Zhang,Xiaocheng Lu,Haobin Zhong,Xiaotong Zhao,Alan Zhao,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出WorldCycle，一种自验证的强化学习框架，利用可逆动作循环为零样本监督的信号，通过空间闭合奖励和时间一致性奖励优化视频世界模型，将状态回归漂移降低44%，复合动作准确率提升近4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式视频世界模型在长期规划和探索中至关重要，但存在累积误差问题。后训练方法（如强化学习）虽能改善模型，却面临验证瓶颈：对于任意动作序列，不存在真实未来的状态可用来衡量长期漂移，导致无法有效监督模型的长期正确性。

Method: 核心洞察是可逆动作循环使验证成为可能：由动作序列与其逆序列组成的序列必须从分析上回归初始状态，从而提供无需标注的长期正确性监督。在此基础上，WorldCycle构建封闭动作循环及其重复执行，并优化两个互补的奖励：1) 空间闭合奖励，强制镜像的前向和反向段之间的对称性；2) 时间一致性奖励，对齐重复循环执行中的状态。这些奖励迫使模型将动作视为一致的状态算子而非记忆的时间模式，并可自然扩展到基模型难以处理的分发外复合动作循环。

Result: 作者还发布了CycleBench基准测试，用于诊断复杂动作结构下的状态返回能力。实验表明WorldCycle将状态回归漂移降低多达44%，复合动作准确率相对基模型提升近4倍。

Conclusion: WorldCycle为物理基础的世界模型提供了关键基础，通过利用动作循环的固有可逆性实现自验证训练，解决了世界模型长期累积误差的核心问题。

Abstract: Interactive video world models are essential for long-horizon planning and exploration, yet they suffer from compounding errors. Post-training methods such as reinforcement learning (RL) can improve these models, but they hit a verification bottleneck: for arbitrary action sequences, no ground-truth future state exists to measure long-term drift. Our key insight is that reversible action cycles make this verification possible: a sequence composed with its inverse must analytically return to the initial state, yielding annotation-free supervision on long-horizon correctness. Building on this, we introduce WorldCycle, a self-verifiable RL framework that constructs closed action cycles and their repeated executions from ordinary action sequences, and optimizes two complementary rewards: a spatial closure reward enforcing symmetry between mirrored forward and reverse segments, and a temporal consistency reward aligning states across repeated cycle executions. These rewards force the model to learn actions as consistent state operators rather than memorized temporal patterns, and extend naturally to out-of-distribution composite action cycles that the base model handles poorly. We further release CycleBench, a diagnostic benchmark for state-returning ability under complex action structures. WorldCycle reduces state returning drift by up to 44% and lifts composite-action accuracy nearly 4x over the base model, providing a vital foundation for physically grounded world models.

</details>


### [145] [Short-term load forecasting under EU-AI Act Requirements in Safety-Critical Environments: Results from a 41-day live challenge on the aggregated German transmission-grid load](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05018)
*Thomas Bartz-Beielstein*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个为期41天的实时挑战赛，评估了基于开源Python库spotforecast2-safe构建的德国输电电网短期负荷预测（STLF）完整流水线。该流水线遵循EU-AI法案在安全关键环境中的设计要求，融合异常检测、间隙感知数据处理、日历与天气协变量、递归多步预测及超参数调优，在准确性上超越了官方ENTSO-E日前预测基线，并证明了透明、低成本、可审计的本地模型（macl2l）可媲美超大规模预训练基础模型（如chronos-2）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短期负荷预测在电力行业至关重要，被欧洲和德国法律指定为关键基础设施。随着EU-AI法案的出台，STLF不再仅仅是精度问题，还涉及软件工程和合规性问题——确定性、可复现性和可审计性成为工程必需而非可选。作者希望展示一个既满足合规要求又具备竞争力的完整STLF流水线。

Method: 该流水线基于开源Python库spotforecast2-safe构建，该库按设计实现了EU-AI法案在安全关键环境中的要求。预测流程包括：从ENTSO-E数据中预测目标日24小时的负荷值、异常检测、间隙感知数据准备、日历和天气协变量、递归多步预测算法以及超参数调优。通过41天实时挑战赛对完整流水线进行评估，并对比官方ENTSO-E日前预测基线和大型预训练基础模型chronos-2。

Result: EU-AI法案合规的spotforecast2-safe流水线在预测精度上超越了ENTSO-E官方基线。上下文内模型展现出竞争性表现。透明、低成本、可审计的本地模型（macl2l）与拥有超过一亿参数、高能耗的预训练基础模型chronos-2表现相当。挑战基础设施、所有团队的完整提交历史及冻结的最终排行榜均可公开获取。

Conclusion: 研究证明，符合EU-AI法案合规要求的本地轻量级模型可以在短期负荷预测中达到甚至超越官方基线和大型基础模型的精度水平，表明透明性和可审计性与卓越性能并非互斥。该工作为电力行业在法规合规约束下构建可信赖的预测系统提供了实证依据和可复用的开源基础设施。

Abstract: Short-term load forecasting (STLF) play a vital role in the electric power industry. It serves infrastructure that European and German law designate as critical. Determinism, reproducibility, and auditability are engineering requirements rather than optional extras. STLF is no longer purely an accuracy problem. It is also a software-engineering and compliance problem. This paper describes results from a 41-day live challenge that evaluated a complete STLF pipeline for the aggregated German transmission-grid load. The pipeline is based on the open-source Python library spotforecast2-safe, which implements the EU-AI Act Requirements in Safety-Critical Environments by design. The pipeline predicts the 24 hourly load values of a target day from European Network of Transmission System Operators for Electricity (ENTSO-E) data. It includes anomaly detection and gap-aware data preparation, calendar and weather covariates, a recursive multi-step forecasting algorithm, and hyperparameter tuning. Forecast accuracy is measured against the official ENTSO-E day-ahead forecast. The EU-AI act compliant spotforecast2-safe pipeline beats the ENTSO-E baseline. In-context models show competitive performance. Transparent, low-cost, and auditable local models (referred to as macl2l in this paper) are competitive with more than 100-million-parameter large, energy-intensive pre-trained foundation models such as chronos-2. The challenge infrastructure, the complete submission history of all teams, and the frozen final leaderboard are publicly available.

</details>


### [146] [From Score Matrices to Football-Aware Match-State Simulation: An Auditable LLM Harness for Exact-Score Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05030)
*Shaopeng Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可审计的足球比分预测混合架构，将动态Poisson族统计模型（Dixon-Coles基线）与LLM情境推理相结合，经过四个迭代版本（V1-V4）逐步引入LLM上下文评分映射、逐球仿真、突破后级联判断与确定性尾部候选，并在2025-26英超前150场回放中验证，最终V4达到14.7% Top-1和30.7% Top-3精确比分准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态Poisson族模型擅长估计球队实力、期望进球和连贯的比分概率，但无法理解角色、战术对位、动机或首球后行为变化；LLM能推理这些概念但并非校准概率引擎。作者旨在结合两者，构建一个可审计的信息编排架构，以改善比分预测与选择。

Method: 构建"信息编排器"（information harness），定义输入语义、提供赛前证据并约束LLM走可检查的推理路径。四个迭代：V1为动态得分驱动Dixon-Coles基线；V2将LLM上下文评分映射回期望进球参数；V3以逐球仿真替代标量修正，在冻结的比分候选集上生成比分；V4增加共享首破与进球后级联判断、时间感知停止和确定性尾部候选。

Result: 在2025-26英超前150场时间顺序回放中，V1达到10.0% Top-1和26.7% Top-3精确比分准确率；V3达12.0%和30.0%；V4达14.7%和30.7%。V4将候选覆盖率从77.3%提升到84.7%，但新增尾部候选无一成为Top-3精确命中。V1原生1X2分布达53.3% argmax准确率、0.9878对数损失、0.5870 Brier分数和0.2095排序概率分数。

Conclusion: 该研究的贡献在于可审计的混合架构、清晰的设计演进，以及展示足球感知仿真在比分选择上哪些方面有效、哪些无效的负面发现。结果属于探索性质，因为开发切片并非未触碰的基准，且时间输入隔离无法排除封闭LLM中的结果记忆。

Abstract: Football score forecasting combines a strong statistical core with a difficult contextual edge. Dynamic Poisson-family models estimate team strength, expected goals, and coherent score probabilities, but do not directly understand roles, tactical matchups, motivation, or how a first goal changes behaviour. Large language models (LLMs) can reason about such concepts, yet are not calibrated probability engines. We combine both components through an auditable information harness. This paper documents four iterations: V1, a dynamic score-driven Dixon-Coles baseline; V2, which maps LLM contextual ratings back into expected-goal parameters; V3, which replaces scalar correction with goal-by-goal simulations over a frozen score-candidate set; and V4, which adds shared first-breakthrough and post-goal cascade judgments, time-aware stopping, and deterministic tail candidates. The harness defines input semantics, supplies pre-match evidence, and constrains the LLM to an inspectable reasoning route. On a chronological replay of the first 150 matches of the 2025-26 English Premier League, V1 achieved 10.0% Top-1 and 26.7% Top-3 exact-score accuracy. V3 reached 12.0% and 30.0%, while V4 reached 14.7% and 30.7%. V4 increased candidate coverage from 77.3% to 84.7%, although no added tail candidate became a Top-3 exact hit. V1's native 1X2 distribution achieved 53.3% argmax accuracy, 0.9878 log loss, 0.5870 Brier score, and 0.2095 ranked probability score. These results are exploratory: the development slice is not an untouched benchmark, and temporal input isolation cannot exclude outcome memory in a closed LLM. The contribution is an auditable hybrid architecture, a clear design evolution, and negative findings showing where football-aware simulation does and does not improve score selection.

</details>


### [147] [Item Response Theory for AI Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05086)
*Joshua Fonseca Rivera,Neil Shah,David Demitri Africa,Konstantinos Voudouris*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文使用项目反应理论（IRT）对8个安全基准、192个语言模型进行大规模心理测量分析，发现三个可解释因子（拒绝严格性、真实性、情境危害）解释模型间的大部分差异；心理测量学选择的项目能以更低误差恢复完整基准分数，约10个自适应选择的项目即可覆盖多个单一基准（成本降低97-99%）；IRT还可用于检测模型层面的朴素沙袋行为及API后模型变更。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统安全基准的聚合得分难以信任和解释，原因在于基准之间相互重复、高度相关，且模型可能在检测到评估时进行沙袋（sandbagging）行为，需要一种更可靠的统计方法来测量语言模型的安全行为潜在特质。

Method: 采用项目反应理论（IRT），对8个安全基准、192个语言模型的评估结果进行心理测量建模，拟合潜在因子结构，进行项目选择与自适应测试，并利用IRT进行模型层面的审计（包括检测沙袋行为和API后模型变更）。

Result: 1）发现拒绝严格性、真实性、情境危害三个可解释因子主导模型间差异；2）心理测量学选择的项目比随机子集误差更低，约10个自适应项目即可覆盖单一基准，成本降低97-99%；3）IRT能够检测朴素沙袋行为及API后模型变更。

Conclusion: IRT是现成的工具集，适用于安全基准的解读、精简和审计，建议前沿实验室和评估方采用。

Abstract: Language models differ in how safely they behave and these differences are measured by safety benchmarks. But aggregated benchmark scores are hard to trust and interpret, because benchmarks duplicate one another, correlate heavily, and models may sandbag when they detect evaluation. To address these issues, we draw on Item Response Theory (IRT), a statistical toolkit for measuring these latents from performance on items with inferred psychometric properties. We fit IRT models to eight safety benchmarks across 192 language models, the largest psychometric analysis of LLM safety evaluations to date, and contribute three results. First, we find that three interpretable factors of refusal strictness, truthfulness, and contextual harm explain most of the variance between models across benchmarks. Second, psychometrically selected items recover full benchmark scores with lower error than random subsets of the same size, and roughly ten adaptively chosen items suffice for several individual benchmarks, cutting evaluation cost by 97-99%. Third, IRT supports audits of individual models, showing that it can be used to detect naive sandbagging and changes of model behind APIs. Overall, we show IRT is a ready-made toolkit for reading, reducing, and auditing safety benchmarks, which we recommend frontier labs and evaluators adopt.

</details>


### [148] [Hierarchical Graph Memory for LLM Agents with Path-level Localization and Rewrite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05095)
*Xiawei Yue,Boran Wang,Xiaoqing Zhang,Shuxin Zheng,Ziwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HiGram，一种演进的分层图记忆框架，通过路径级定位与协同重写机制解决长期推理中智能体记忆的检索效率与更新一致性问题，在长时对话问答和冲突感知记忆评测中显著提升答案质量和token效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图记忆的方法将所有记忆存储在扁平图结构中，存在两个核心问题：(1) 随着时间推移积累的历史记忆会引入大量不相关上下文，增加检索时证据选择的成本；(2) 记忆单元通常被独立更新，当相关变化发生时需要重复进行单元级的重写操作，效率低下且难以保证一致性。

Method: HiGram包含三个核心组件：(1) 分层图记忆架构：将记忆组织为包含上层节点和MemoryUnits的由粗到细的层级结构，减少检索时的无关信息量；(2) 基于MicroGraph的路径级定位：利用查询和更新条件化的MicroGraph识别支持子图和证据路径，在重写前完成精细定位；(3) 协同重写方法：联合修订单元内记忆与单元间依赖关系，确保在定位的证据路径上更新有效的依赖结构。

Result: 在长期对话问答基准和冲突感知记忆评测上的实验表明，HiGram在答案质量和token效率上显著优于基线方法；在动态、静态和条件性冲突场景下，该方法均能提升答案准确性和查询有效的证据选择能力。

Conclusion: HiGram通过分层组织记忆并结合路径级定位与协同重写，有效解决了扁平图记忆在长期推理中检索效率低和更新不一致的难题，为面向长期推理的智能体记忆系统提供了新的设计范式。

Abstract: Agents for long term reasoning require a memory that can be efficiently and effectively updated over time, as new facts and external feedback continue to arrive. Recently, graph memory has been adopted to offer structural organization for multi-hop retrieval and reasoning. However, existing methods store all memories in a flat graph, and accumulated historical memories can introduce irrelevant contexts and increase the cost of evidence selection during retrieval. Moreover, they typically update memory units independently, requiring repeated unit-wise rewrite to cover related changes. To address these issues, we propose HiGram, an evolving hierarchical graph memory framework with path-level localization and rewriting. Specifically, we first propose a hierarchical graph memory, which organizes the memory into coarse-to-fine architecture composed of upper-level nodes and MemoryUnits, thereby reducing the amount of irrelevant information during retrieval. We further propose MicroGraph-based path-level localization, which leverages query and update conditioned MicroGraphs to identify support subgraph and evidence path before rewrite. Finally, we propose a coordinated rewriting method that jointly revises intra-unit memory and inter-unit dependencies, enable valid dependency structures updating in the localized evidence path. Experiments on benchmarks for long-term conversational question answering and conflict-aware memory evaluation demonstrate that our method demonstrate substantial improvements over baselines in answer quality and token efficiency. Besides, our method improves answer accuracy and query-valid evidence selection under dynamic, static, and conditional conflicts.

</details>


### [149] [ABSeeker: Training Long-Horizon Search Agents via Answer-Backtracked Credit Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05102)
*Yijun Lu,Rui Ye,Jiajun Wang,Yuwen Du,Tian Jin,Songhua Liu,Siheng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ABC（Answer-Backtracked Credit Assignment）框架，将稀疏的轨迹级搜索结果转化为密集的步骤级监督信号，用于训练长程搜索智能体。通过答案回溯线索恢复和线索锚定步骤评分机制，在仅用8.5k样本训练Qwen3.5-4B的基础上，在BrowseComp和BrowseComp-ZH基准上分别达到37.3%和39.1%的准确率，配合上下文管理后可提升至55.3%和52.9%，显著超越同规模智能体并匹敌约30B参数的大型模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在训练长程搜索智能体时，将轨迹中所有步骤视为同等重要，无论是SFT还是RL阶段都无法区分有效步骤、错误步骤和冗余步骤。这种均匀处理方式导致模型无法从失败轨迹中学习有价值的行为，也无法有效抑制搜索过程中的噪声行动。

Method: 1) Answer-Backtracked Clue Recovery：从最终答案回溯推理，恢复求解问题所需的中间线索；2) Clue-Anchored Step Scoring：以恢复的线索为锚点评估每个搜索步骤的相关性，将稀疏的二值结果监督转化为密集的步骤级奖励；3) 基于这些步骤级奖励构建ABC-SFT（按步骤重加权损失）和ABC-GRPO（以步骤级分数作为GRPO奖励）。

Result: ABSeeker（基于Qwen3.5-4B，仅8.5k训练样本）在BrowseComp上达到37.3%，在BrowseComp-ZH上达到39.1%；加入上下文管理后进一步提升至55.3%和52.9%，显著优于同规模（4B）智能体，并达到约30B参数模型的性能水平。

Conclusion: 答案回溯的步骤级信用分配对于训练长程搜索智能体是有效的，即使是在失败的轨迹中也能提取有用步骤的奖励，并通过抑制错误和冗余行为提升整体搜索性能。

Abstract: Long-horizon search agents must make multiple sequential actions (steps) to search, retrieve, verify, and integrate evidence to reach a final answer. However, existing methods for training these agents typically treat all steps within a trajectory uniformly during both supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL), failing to distinguish useful actions from erroneous or redundant ones. In this paper, we propose Answer-Backtracked Credit Assignment (ABC), a fine-grained credit assignment framework for training long-horizon search agents by converting sparse trajectory-level outcomes into dense step-level supervision that rewards useful actions (even in failed trajectories) while suppressing erroneous or redundant actions. Specifically, given a potentially obscure query and its corresponding ground-truth answer, ABC first performs Answer-Backtracked Clue Recovery, which traces back from the answer to recover intermediate clues required to solve the question. It then applies Clue-Anchored Step Scoring to evaluate each search step against these clues, converting sparse binary outcome supervision into dense step-level rewards. Based on these rewards, we develop ABC-SFT, which reweights the loss of each turn, and ABC-GRPO, which uses the step-level scores as rewards in GRPO. Building on this framework, we train ABSeeker based on Qwen3.5-4B with only 8.5k examples. ABSeeker achieves 37.3% on BrowseComp and 39.1% on BrowseComp-ZH. With context management, the scores further improve to 55.3% and 52.9%, respectively, significantly outperforming same-scale (4B) agents and even matching the performance of larger ones (approximately 30B). These results demonstrate the effectiveness of answer-backtracked step-level credit assignment for training long-horizon search agents.

</details>


### [150] [CoPlan: A Trustworthy Co-Intelligence Interface for Care Planning through Role-Based Contestable Argument Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05107)
*Hung Truong Thanh Nguyen,Hélène Fournier,Piper Jackson,Makoto Itoh,Shannon Freeman,Rene Richard,Hung Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍CoPlan，一个面向人机协作护理规划的"共同智能且可争议"接口。CoPlan采用多智能体工作流，由专门的AI智能体生成候选干预方案及支持/挑战性论证，人类护理规划者可在最终方案生成前对论证进行接受、拒绝、修改或补充，从而实现人类与AI的互补协作（共同智能）与推荐方案的可检查、可修订和可论证（可争议性）。系统在居家养老护理规划场景中验证，支持自适应用户团队招募、基于角色的论证审查、最终护理方案生成及调度智能体跟进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI辅助护理规划系统大多将推荐作为固定输出呈现，限制了利益相关者（临床医生、患者、照护者、护理团队）在推荐与临床判断、患者价值观或现实可行性冲突时进行检查、质疑和修订方案的能力，缺乏人类能动性和临床问责机制。

Method: CoPlan采用多智能体工作流架构：专门化的AI智能体生成候选干预措施及支持性或挑战性论证；人类护理规划者可在最终方案生成前对论证进行接受、拒绝、修改或添加。系统将"共同智能"（人类与AI互补贡献专业知识）与"可争议性"（推荐保持开放供检查、修订和论证）相结合。

Result: 系统在居家养老护理规划场景中进行了演示，支持自适应用户团队招募、基于角色的论证审查、最终护理方案生成，以及通过调度智能体实现的实际后续跟进。

Conclusion: 该工作贡献了一个可争议的护理规划接口及一个可信人机护理规划的设计框架，该框架在保留人类能动性和临床问责制的同时，提升了AI在复杂护理决策中的可用性与可信度。

Abstract: AI-supported care planning can help clinicians, patients, caregivers, and care teams coordinate complex decisions across clinical, functional, psychosocial, and environmental needs. However, many AI systems present recommendations as fixed outputs, limiting stakeholders' ability to inspect, challenge, and revise plans when they conflict with clinical judgment, patient values, or real-world feasibility. We present CoPlan - a Co-Intelligent and Contestable Interface for Human-AI Care Planning. CoPlan uses a multi-agent workflow in which specialized AI agents generate candidate interventions and supporting or challenging arguments, while human care planners can accept, reject, modify, or add arguments before final plan generation. Through this design, CoPlan combines co-intelligence, in which humans and AI agents contribute complementary expertise, with contestability, where recommendations remain open to inspection, revision, and justification. We demonstrate CoPlan in an aging-in-place care planning scenario. The system supports adaptive care team recruitment, role-based argument review, final care plan generation, and practical follow-up through scheduling agents. This work contributes a contestable care planning interface and a design framing for trustworthy human-AI care planning that preserves human agency and clinical accountability.

</details>


### [151] [OctoLong: Mid-Training On Cross-Repository Code Contexts Enhances Long-Context Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05141)
*Indraneil Paul,Falko Helm,Goran Glavaš,Iryna Gurevych*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OctoLong，一种上下文工程流水线，通过AST解析器、语言服务器后端和包管理器递归检索代码引用，构建百万级token的依赖丰富的代码上下文数据集。基于此训练了OctoLong-Instruct系列模型（600M到14B参数），在约50B token混合数据上进行上下文扩展中训练和指令微调，显著提升长距离检索、长期状态跟踪、仓库级代码理解和智能体任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长上下文语料主要由书籍、学术文章和代码仓库构成，这些资源有限且缺乏长距离依赖关系，制约了语言模型在长上下文场景（如上下文学习、自我改进、长时程智能体工作流）中的能力提升。需要一种能生成富含长距离依赖关系上下文数据的方法。

Method: 设计OctoLong上下文工程流水线，集成AST解析器、语言服务器后端和包管理器，实现代码引用的递归检索，从而构建数百万token的依赖密集型代码上下文。然后用包含约6.2B token OctoLong代码上下文的约50B token混合数据对基础模型（600M到14B参数）进行上下文扩展中训练，再经过约10B token的指令微调。

Result: 与18个最先进的开源权重长上下文语言模型对比及训练消融实验表明，仅用OctoLong数据替代传统上下文扩展语料的12%，即可在长距离检索、长期状态跟踪、仓库级代码理解和下游智能体任务上获得显著提升，同时还能增强短上下文编码场景中的API使用能力。

Conclusion: OctoLong通过上下文工程技术利用代码的依赖结构生成高质量长上下文训练数据，以较低的数据替换比例即可大幅提升模型的长期上下文能力，验证了在长上下文训练中以代码依赖数据替代传统语料的可行性，并为未来的上下文扩展研究提供了新方向。

Abstract: Context lengths of language models (LMs) have dramatically increased, driven by the demands for in-context learning, self-improvement, and long-horizon agentic workflows. Existing long-context corpora, however, are dominated by books, academic articles, and code repositories, which are finite resources and often scarce in long-distance dependencies. In this work, we introduce OctoLong, a context engineering pipeline that instruments an AST parser, a language server backend, and a package manager to facilitate the recursive retrieval of code references, enabling the curation of dependency-rich code contexts of millions of tokens in length. We then train OctoLong-Instruct, a suite of capable long-context open LMs, derived from base models ranging in size from 600M to 14B parameters, via context-extension mid-training on a ~50B-token mixture containing ~6.2B tokens of OctoLong code contexts, followed by ~10B tokens of instruction tuning. Our training ablations and experimental evaluations against 18 state-of-the-art open-weight long-context LMs show that supplanting just 12% of traditional context-extension corpora with OctoLong data yields substantial gains in long-range retrieval, long-term state tracking, repository-level code understanding, and downstream agentic tasks, while also enhancing API usage in short-context coding scenarios.

</details>


### [152] [Argus: A General-Purpose Agentic Runtime for Long-Horizon Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05144)
*Boxiu Li,Zimo Wen,Yijia Fan,Junxiang Lei,Sufeng Guo,Jiaao Wu,Ruize Tang,Mukai Li,Yifei Shen,Xiaoyu Chen,Wanbo Zhang,Runjing Gu,Yifei Gao,Yuheng Wu,Xuyao Huang,Zelong Zhao,Jiachen Zhang,Shibo Hu,Hangxi Guo,Yilin Chen,Yuzhe Zhang,Fan Yang,Chuan Wen,Xian Zhang,Xuanhe Zhou,Zhijie Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Argus，一个持久化、自演化的智能体运行时系统，通过Manager、Planner、Engineer和Reviewer四个角色在持久化项目状态上执行有界任务。Argus将稳定的用户意图与操作目标、约束和验证标准分离，仅在角色审查和任务原生验证通过后才接纳记忆、技能、流程、验证器、路由决策和拒绝路径。模型权重保持不变，自演化通过持久化运行时状态和控制策略实现。在七个GPT-5.5基准竞技场中，Argus在SWE-Bench Pro上达到约78%准确率（对比Direct Copilot的59%），仅使用1.41倍的聚合token。经过验证门控的自演化后，成熟SWE-Bench批次相比启动批次减少21%的求解输入token和15%的活跃工作流时间，记录34次验证器恢复和22次严格审查循环救援。Argus在AARRI-Bench上达到76.8%，数学数据综合上拉出28.0分差距，GPU内核和语言模型训练结果具竞争力。超越基准之外，优化后的RWKV6内核已上游合并；多日数学战役保留被证伪的路径和有证明支持的前沿更新；六个论文流水线完成254个任务，16次阶段回滚。结果表明固定权重、自演化的框架能够修订、恢复和积累经验证的方法，同时为未来的监督学习和强化学习产生结构化轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视野推理需要一个智能体运行时，当证据支持当前方法时能够坚持，当测量揭示失败、隐藏约束或目标设定错误时能够转向。现有系统缺乏这种持久性和自适应能力，因此需要一种能够分离稳定用户意图与操作目标、并在验证门控下自我演化的运行时框架。

Method: Argus由四个核心角色组成：Manager、Planner、Engineer和Reviewer，在持久化项目状态上执行有界任务。系统将稳定用户意图与操作目标、约束和验证标准相分离，所有新知识（记忆、技能、程序、验证器、路由决策和被拒绝的路径）必须经过角色拥有的审查和任务原生验证后才能被接纳。模型权重固定，自演化通过持久化运行时状态和控制策略发生，操作员拥有升级点之间的自主执行。

Result: 在SWE-Bench Pro上达到约78%（对比Direct Copilot的59%），仅使用1.41倍聚合token；AARRI-Bench上达到76.8%；数学数据综合上实现28.0分差距；GPU内核和语言模型训练结果具竞争力。自演化带来21%更少的求解输入token和15%更少的活跃工作流时间，记录34次验证器恢复和22次严格审查循环救援。RWKV6内核已上游合并，数学战役保留被证伪路径，六个论文流水线完成254个任务和16次阶段回滚。

Conclusion: 固定权重、自演化的框架能够有效修订、恢复和积累经过验证的方法，在多个基准上超越直接基线，同时产生结构化轨迹，可为未来的监督学习和强化学习提供训练数据。

Abstract: Long-horizon reasoning requires an agentic runtime that can persist when evidence supports its current approach and pivot when measurements reveal failure, hidden constraints, or a misspecified objective. We present Argus, a persistent, self-evolving runtime in which Manager, Planner, Engineer, and Reviewer execute bounded missions over durable project state. Argus separates stable user intent from operational objectives, constraints, and verification criteria, and admits memories, skills, procedures, verifiers, routing decisions, and rejected routes only after role-owned review and, when available, task-native verification. Model weights remain fixed; self-evolution occurs through persistent runtime state and control policy, with autonomous execution between operator-owned escalation points. Across seven GPT-5.5 benchmark arenas, Argus achieves about 78% on SWE-Bench Pro versus 59% for Direct Copilot while using 1.41 times the aggregate tokens. After verification-gated self-evolution, mature SWE-Bench waves use 21% fewer solve-input tokens and 15% less active workflow time per task than startup waves, while recording 34 verifier recoveries and 22 strict review-loop rescues. Argus also reaches 76.8% on AARRI-Bench and a 28.0-point gap on mathematical data synthesis, with competitive GPU-kernel and language-model-training results. Beyond benchmarks, an optimized RWKV6 kernel was merged upstream; a multi-day mathematics campaign retained falsified routes and proof-backed frontier updates; and six paper pipelines completed 254 missions with 16 stage rollbacks. These results show that a fixed-weight, self-evolving harness can revise, recover, and accumulate verified approaches while producing structured trajectories for future supervised and reinforcement learning.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [153] [Statistical learning theory and Occam's razor: Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04049)
*Tom F. Sterkenburg*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文从统计学习理论角度为奥卡姆剃刀原则（正则化/简单性偏好）提供了一个方法论层面的正当性辩护，论证了为获得理论可靠性和"所见即所得"保证，必须偏好简单性而非拟合度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奥卡姆剃刀原则（偏好简单性）在科学哲学和机器学习中都被广泛讨论，但一直缺乏令人信服的正当性证明。已有论证要么退化为纯粹实用主义（为简单而简单），要么依赖于本体论假设（真理本身是简单的），两者都不令人满意。本文旨在提供一个真正的方法论正当性论证。

Method: 基于此前提出的"核心论证"（core argument），从统计学习理论（statistical learning theory）出发，为"正则化"（regularization）程序——即在拟合度与简单性之间进行权衡——提供一种"手段-目的"（means-ends）论证。核心思路是：要获得理论可靠性和"What-You-See-Is-What-You-Get"（所见即所得）的保证，就必须实施对简单性相对于拟合度的偏好。

Result: 论证结果表明，奥卡姆剃刀原则可以作为一种真正的方法论正当性得到辩护。这种辩护既不会退化为纯粹实用主义的"为简单而偏好简单"原则，也不依赖于"真理是简单的"这一本体论假设。统计学习理论提供了严格的数学基础来支撑这一论证。

Conclusion: 正则化（即偏好简单性胜过拟合度）可以从统计学习理论中获得方法论层面上的严格正当性：为了获得可靠的泛化保证，我们必须在归纳推断中实施对简单性的系统偏好。这一论证同时避免了将奥卡姆剃刀归结为纯实用原则或本体论假设的两个极端。

Abstract: The principle of Occam's razor, which instructs us to prefer simplicity in inductive inference, has attracted much scrutiny both in the philosophy of science and in machine learning. In either field, however, a justification for the principle has been elusive. In this paper, building on an earlier "core argument," I spell out a justification from statistical learning theory for the procedure of regularization: for trading off fit for simplicity. The means-ends argument is that in order to profit from theoretical reliability and "what-you-see-is-what-you-get" guarantees, one must implement a certain preference for simplicity over fit. This is a genuine methodological justification, which neither collapses to a purely pragmatic principle that we prefer simplicity for its own sake, nor to an ontological assumption that the truth is simple.

</details>


### [154] [Automatic Statistical Test for Rationally Expressible Algorithms by Selective Inference, with Applications to Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04667)
*Teruyuki Katsuoka,Tomohiro Shiraishi,Shuichi Nishino,Ichiro Takeuchi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: AutoSI是一个自动化的选择性推断（Selective Inference, SI）框架，它通过自动从算法的单个操作中构建选择事件（selection event），将精确SI的适用范围从线性/二次不等式扩展到任何可用数据的有理函数（多项式之比）表达的算法。用户只需以普通NumPy风格代码编写算法，无需手工推导。论文证明AutoSI生成的p-值在有限样本下精确有效，并展示了其在包括交叉验证选取lasso正则化参数在内的特征选择方法上的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确选择性推断（exact SI）在统计上是有效的，但为每个新算法开发SI程序需要专家手工推导并实现其选择事件（即假设被选中的数据条件）。这种专门化工作重复进行，导致精确SI目前仅适用于很狭窄的算法类别。传统方法仅能处理由线性或二次不等式刻画的选择事件，缺乏自动化和通用的框架。

Method: AutoSI框架包含两个核心贡献：(1) 自动构建选择事件——从算法的每个个体操作（以NumPy风格代码编写）中自动提取选择条件，用户无需手工推导任何细节；(2) 扩展选择事件的类别——现有精确方法限于线性或二次不等式，而AutoSI支持任何可用数据的有理函数（多项式之比）表达的算法，从而覆盖更广泛的算法类。AutoSI计算出的p-值被证明在有限样本下精确有效。

Result: 论文在三个特征选择方法上进行了演示，每个方法仅需几十行代码实现。其中一种方法——通过交叉验证R²选取正则化参数的lasso——无法被现有精确SI框架处理，但AutoSI使其成为可能。在合成和真实数据上的实验表明，AutoSI生成的p-值能够将第一类错误率（假阳性率）控制在名义水平，同时保持较高的统计功效。

Conclusion: AutoSI通过自动构建选择事件和扩展可处理的算法类别，消除了精确选择性推断的主要障碍，使SI方法能够以用户友好的方式应用于更广泛的统计算法，同时保持有限样本下的精确有效性。

Abstract: Selective inference (SI) provides statistically valid $p$-values for hypotheses selected by applying an algorithm to the data, correcting for the bias that arises when the same data are used both to select and to test a hypothesis. Developing an SI procedure for a new algorithm, however, has required an expert to derive, and then implement, the selection event, i.e., the conditions under which the hypothesis is selected. Repeating this specialized effort for every new algorithm is why exact SI has so far been available for only a narrow class. We propose AutoSI, a framework that removes this barrier in two ways. First, AutoSI constructs the selection event automatically from the algorithm's individual operations, so the user only writes the algorithm as ordinary NumPy-like code and derives nothing by hand. Second, AutoSI broadens the class of selection events SI can handle: existing exact methods are limited to selection events characterized by linear or quadratic inequalities in the data, whereas AutoSI covers any algorithm expressible through rational functions of the data (ratios of polynomials). We prove that the $p$-values computed by AutoSI are exactly valid in finite samples. We demonstrate AutoSI on three feature-selection methods, each written in a few dozen lines of code. One of these methods, the lasso with its tuning parameter selected by cross-validated $R^2$, cannot be handled within existing exact SI frameworks and is made possible by AutoSI. Experiments on synthetic and real datasets show that the resulting $p$-values control the type I error rate (i.e., the false positive rate) at the nominal level while retaining high power.

</details>


### [155] [Intrinsic-Hybrid Latent Diffusion Models for Generative Modeling on Unknown Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04827)
*Yizhu Wang,Mu Niu,Xiaochen Yang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出内在混合潜变量扩散模型（ILDM），通过将潜空间解释为未知黎曼流形的坐标图，并结合概率解码器量化几何与不确定性，在流形与欧几里得动力学之间基于局部不确定性进行混合扩散，实现几何感知的高质量数据生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准扩散模型在高维数据合成上表现优异但依赖大规模训练数据且忽略内在几何结构；现有潜变量扩散模型虽降低维度却强加欧几里得结构，无法捕捉底层流形几何，在数据稀疏场景下尤为不利。

Method: ILDM将潜空间视为未知黎曼流形的一个坐标图，通过概率解码器量化几何与不确定性；前向过程为混合扩散，基于局部不确定性在黎曼动力学与欧几里得动力学之间切换，其中黎曼分量由解码器导出的概率度量张量控制；引入适应混合扩散设置的近似去噪得分匹配方法，实现由混合Langevin动力学定义的反向过程。

Result: 在COIL-100、MNIST和心脏MRI数据集上的实验表明，ILDM相比标准扩散模型和潜变量扩散模型获得了更低的FID和LPIPS分数，显著提升了生成质量。

Conclusion: 通过将几何结构信息显式融入潜空间建模，ILDM在数据稀疏场景下能够更有效地捕捉底层流形几何，从而超越传统的欧几里得潜空间扩散模型，实现更优的生成性能。

Abstract: We introduce the Intrinsic Hybrid Latent Diffusion Model (ILDM), a generative framework that integrates probabilistic dimensionality reduction with geometry-aware diffusion on unknown manifolds. While diffusion models (DMs) have achieved state-of-the-art results in high-dimensional data synthesis, they rely on large training datasets and ignore intrinsic geometric structure. Latent diffusion models (LDMs) address the high dimensionality by learning a latent space, but they typically impose a Euclidean structure, failing to capture the underlying manifold geometry, especially problematic in data-sparse regimes. ILDM addresses these limitations by interpreting the latent space as a chart of an unknown Riemannian manifold, with geometry and uncertainty quantified through a probabilistic decoder. The forward process is a hybrid diffusion that switches between Riemannian and Euclidean dynamics based on local uncertainty, where the Riemannian component is governed by a probabilistic metric tensor derived from the decoder. To learn the generative dynamics, we introduce an approximate denoising score matching method tailored to the hybrid diffusion setting, enabling a backward process defined by hybrid Langevin dynamics. Experiments on COIL-100, MNIST, and cardiac MRI datasets demonstrate that ILDM significantly improves generation quality, achieving lower FID and LPIPS scores compared to standard diffusion and latent diffusion models.

</details>


### [156] [Stable Density Ridges: Consistency and Convergence of Subspace Constrained Mean Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05112)
*Wanli Qiao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文发现Subspace Constrained Mean Shift (SCMS)算法的轨迹实际上并不收敛于文献中经典定义的"静态脊线"，而是收敛于一种新的几何结构——"稳定脊线"。作者通过动力学系统视角重新定义SCMS的理论目标，并在此基础上提出广义SCMS框架，证明了其统一R-线性收敛性、拓扑满射性，以及Hausdorff距离下的估计收敛率，同时揭示了原算法存在多项式时间计算复杂性的根本原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献中普遍认为SCMS轨迹收敛于经典定义的"静态脊线"，但该定义未能考虑算法底层向量场流动过程中尾随特征空间的旋转效应。作者旨在纠正这一长期存在的理论错误，提供SCMS算法真正收敛目标的正确刻画。

Method: 作者引入"稳定脊线"这一新几何结构，通过投影密度梯度的Jacobian矩阵从动力学系统视角定义它。在此基础上构建使用常数步长的广义SCMS框架，利用动力学系统理论证明其收敛性质，并通过Hausdorff距离推导稳定脊线的估计收敛速率。

Result: 证明了稳定脊线才是SCMS算法的真正理论目标；广义SCMS框架实现统一R-线性收敛和拓扑满射性；给出了稳定脊线的估计收敛率；揭示了原SCMS因Mean Shift算子将步长与平滑带宽隐式耦合导致多项式时间复杂度的缺陷，并展示了广义框架的统计一致性和更高效率。

Conclusion: SCMS算法的理论目标应从"静态脊线"修正为"稳定脊线"；提出的广义SCMS框架在理论上更正确、计算上更高效，为密度脊线提取提供了新的理论范式和实用算法。

Abstract: The Subspace Constrained Mean Shift (SCMS) algorithm is a popular nonparametric method for extracting density ridges, which serve as a low-dimensional representation of high-dimensional data. It is a widely held belief in the literature that SCMS trajectories converge to the classical density ridge, which we call the "static ridge", defined via the density gradient and the eigenvalues and eigenvectors of the density's Hessian. In this paper, we demonstrate that this assumption does not hold in general, as the static definition fails to account for the rotation of the trailing eigenspace along the continuous flow of the algorithm's underlying vector field. To resolve this, we propose a paradigm shift by introducing the "stable ridge", a novel geometric structure defined through the lens of dynamical systems and the Jacobian of the projected density gradient. We prove that this stable ridge is the true theoretical target of the SCMS algorithm. Building upon this foundation, we develop a generalized SCMS framework utilizing a constant step size, establishing its uniform R-linear convergence and topological surjectivity onto the stable ridge. We further derive the rates of convergence for estimating the stable ridge in terms of the Hausdorff distance. Finally, we expose that the original SCMS algorithm suffers from polynomial-time computational complexity, which is caused by implicitly coupling the step size to the smoothing bandwidth via the Mean Shift operator, and demonstrate how our generalized framework provides a statistically consistent and more efficient solution.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [157] [C$^2$MOE: Consistency and Complementarity-guided Mixture of Experts for Incomplete Multimodal Emotion Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04013)
*Yuntao Shou,Tao Meng,Wei Ai,Keqin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出C2MOE，一个基于一致性与互补性引导的混合专家框架，用于处理多模态情感识别对话中模态缺失的问题。该框架通过信息论原理将多模态知识分解为一致性和互补性成分，并采用双分支预测机制和可学习重加权模块实现鲁棒的模态补全，在多个基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MERC方法依赖完整的模态输入，但真实场景中常因传输错误或用户行为导致模态缺失，严重降低模型性能。已有的鲁棒方法通过跨模态一致性学习增强鲁棒性，但忽视了模态间的互补性，导致重建结果存在偏差。

Method: C2MOE采用信息论框架统一表示学习和缺失模态补全。具体包括：(1) 通过交互感知专家将多模态知识分解为一致性和互补性成分，一致性通过最大化跨模态可预测性捕捉，互补性通过最大化模态间条件熵保留；(2) 双分支预测机制：一致性分支通过最小化不确定性将补全特征与联合分布对齐，互补性分支通过熵最大化利用模态特有线索；(3) 可学习重加权模块动态分配各专家输出的重要性分数，实现自适应融合。

Result: 在多个多模态情感识别对话基准上的大量实验表明，C2MOE在多种模态缺失设置下持续优于现有最先进方法，验证了其鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: C2MOE通过同时建模模态间的一致性和互补性，有效解决了现有方法在缺失模态场景下因忽视互补性导致的重建偏差问题，为不完整多模态情感识别提供了更鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in Multimodal Emotion Recognition in Conversations (MERC) highlight its reliance on complete multimodal inputs. However, real-world data often suffer from missing modalities due to transmission errors or user behavior, severely degrading model performance. Existing methods enhance robustness via cross-modal consistency learning but largely ignore modality complementarity, leading to biased reconstructions. To address this limitation, we propose C$2$MOE, a novel Consistency and Complementarity-guided Mixture of Experts framework for incomplete multimodal emotion learning. Our approach unifies representation learning and missing modality imputation within a principled information-theoretic framework. Specifically, multimodal knowledge is factorized into consistency and complementarity components via interaction-aware experts. Consistency is captured by maximizing cross-modal predictability, while complementarity is preserved by maximizing conditional entropy between modalities. Building upon this decomposition, C$2$MOE introduces a dual-branch prediction mechanism for robust imputation under missing modalities. The consistency branch aligns imputed features with the joint distribution by minimizing uncertainty, and the complementarity branch exploits modality-unique cues via entropy maximization. Finally, C$2$MOE employs a learnable reweighting module that dynamically assigns importance scores to each expert's output, yielding a robust and adaptive fusion for imputation. Extensive experiments on multiple MERC benchmarks demonstrate that C$2$MOE consistently surpasses state-of-the-art methods across various missing-modality settings, validating its robustness and generalization.

</details>


### [158] [Informational Frustration in Neural Manifolds: Shannon Bottlenecks and the Limits of Learnability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30512)
*Srinivasa Rao P.,Vangmayi P Reddy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出熵可学习视界（ELH）这一统一框架，将信息论、拓扑学和统计力学联系起来，解释深度网络为何能泛化良好。通过香农-拓扑瓶颈定理揭示了当决策边界几何复杂度超过信息视界时系统发生熵相变，陷入"信息挫败"（玻璃态刚性记忆阶段）。文章还重新诠释了grokking现象为"熵释放"，并提出熵梯度下降（EGD）算法动态管理权重熵以保持学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典理论框架（如VC维、Rademacher复杂度）预测现代深度模型会发生灾难性过拟合，这与实际观察到的良好泛化能力严重矛盾。作者旨在弥合这一理论与现实之间的巨大鸿沟，建立统一的物理学视角来刻画深度学习的能力极限。

Method: 作者建立了一个连接信息论、拓扑学与统计力学的统一框架。核心概念是熵可学习视界（ELH）：当数据流形的香农熵超过决策边界的拓扑熵（并由权重空间的冯·诺依曼熵平衡）时，网络才能真正学习目标函数。基于此提出香农-拓扑瓶颈定理，并推导算法——熵梯度下降（EGD），通过动态管理权重熵来维持学习过程。

Result: 证明了香农-拓扑瓶颈定理：当目标边界几何复杂度超过信息视界时，系统经历突发熵相变，进入"信息挫败"状态——一种玻璃态、刚性记忆（过拟合）阶段，泛化在热力学意义上变得不可能。同时将grokking现象解释为"熵释放"过程，并提出EGD算法作为实践工具。

Conclusion: 熵不仅是追踪不确定性的工具，而是决定机器能否学习的根本物理货币。该工作重新定位了熵在深度学习理论中的核心地位，为泛化问题提供了一个热力学视角的统一解释。

Abstract: Why overparameterised deep networks generalise so remarkably well remains one of the most stubborn open questions in machine learning theory. Classical frameworks like VC dimension and Rademacher complexity predict catastrophic overfitting in modern models, leaving a massive theoretical gap between theory and reality. In this paper, we bridge this divide by introducing a unified framework that links information theory, topology, and statistical mechanics to map the hard limits of deep learning. Central to our approach is the Entropic Learnability Horizon (ELH): a fundamental law stating that a network can only truly learn a target function if the Shannon entropy of the data manifold outpaces the topological entropy of the function's decision boundary, balanced by the von Neumann entropy of the network's weight space. We establish the Shannon-Topological Bottleneck Theorem, proving that when a target boundary's geometric complexity exceeds this informational horizon, the system undergoes a sudden entropic phase transition. It falls into a state of Informational Frustration - a glassy, rigid memorization phase where generalization becomes thermodynamically impossible. Using this lens, we show that the enigmatic phenomenon of "grokking" is actually an Entropic Release, where weights abruptly reorganise to unlock the bottleneck. Finally, we translate this theory into practice with Entropic Gradient Descent (EGD), an optimization algorithm that dynamically manages weight entropy to keep learning on track. Ultimately, this work repositions entropy not just as a tool for tracking uncertainty but as the fundamental physical currency that dictates whether a machine can learn.

</details>


### [159] [On Hamming-Lipschitz Type Stability of the Subdominant (Minmax) Ultrametric: Theory and Simple Proofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04014)
*Alokendu Mazumder,Arnab Roy,Punit Rathore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对次主导（极小极大）超度量算子的稳定性理论进行了ℓ₀范数层面的研究。核心发现是稀疏扰动（仅改动少量成对距离）对超度量值的影响仅通过最小生成树（MST）传播，并给出了每个编辑的暴露切割分数、Hamming-Lipschitz界以及相关的最优性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的超度量稳定性理论通常基于ℓ∞或Gromov-Hausdorff度量，但这些度量不适用于稀疏扰动场景——即仅修改少数几对距离的情况。作者旨在建立一种ℓ₀型（即稀疏性度量）的稳定性理论，以更好地刻画和量化稀疏编辑对层次聚类结构的影响，并为层次表示提供漏洞诊断工具。

Method: 作者系统分析了超度量算子对稀疏扰动的响应机制，建立了一个ℓ₀型稳定性理论。关键分析包括：(1) 证明稀疏编辑仅通过MST传播影响，即成对超度量值变化当且仅当其树路径跨越一个被编辑的边或一个新暴露的切割；(2) 引入每个编辑的暴露切割得分和树专属全局包络；(3) 推导出Hamming-Lipschitz界来限制可变化的超度量条目数量；(4) 通过严格切割分离和离树编辑等构造性例子证明这些界的最优性；(5) 提出条件近可加性原理以处理多重编辑场景。

Result: 本文证明了稀疏编辑仅通过MST传播影响超度量值，给出了每个编辑的逐步精确暴露切割分数和树专属全局包络。证明了在严格切割分离下树边界的精确可达性，并构造了离树编辑实例使单次编辑改变Θ(n²)个超度量条目的显式族。还证明了在认证的大变化区域和可忽略聚合重叠条件下的多重编辑近可加性原理。在深度嵌入图上的实验表明，这些结构性分数为层次表示提供了有效的漏洞诊断。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了超度量算子的ℓ₀稳定性理论，揭示了稀疏扰动传播与MST几何结构之间的本质联系。这种对树几何依赖性的分析不仅是不可避免的（有最优性证明支撑），而且为层次聚类表示的鲁棒性和安全性分析提供了新的理论工具和实用诊断指标。

Abstract: The subdominant (minmax) ultrametric is a canonical tree-structured summary of a dissimilarity matrix, arising equivalently as the ultrametric induced by single-linkage clustering. While its classical stability theory is usually formulated in $\ell_\infty$ or Gromov--Hausdorff terms, such bounds are poorly suited to sparse perturbations that alter only a few pairwise distances. We develop an $\ell_0$-type stability theory for this operator. Our analysis shows that sparse edits propagate only through the minimum spanning tree (MST): a pairwise ultrametric value can change only if its tree path crosses an edited edge or a cut newly exposed by an edited off-tree edge. This yields a sharp per-edit exposed-cut score and a tree-only global envelope, leading to Hamming--Lipschitz bounds on the number of ultrametric entries that can change. We also prove sharpness results showing that this dependence on tree geometry is unavoidable: under strict cut separation the tree-edge bound is attained exactly, and for off-tree edits there are explicit families in which one edited distance changes $Θ(n^2)$ ultrametric entries. In addition, we prove a conditional near-additivity principle for multiple edits under certified large per-edit changed regions and negligible aggregate overlap. Experiments on deep-embedding graphs show that the resulting structural scores provide useful vulnerability diagnostics for hierarchical representations.

</details>


### [160] [Statistical Mechanics of Learning on Product Wasserstein Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01434)
*Srinivasa Rao P Vangmayi P Reddy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将分布约束视为学习发生的内在几何结构，而非对模型容量的限制。通过将深度神经网络和变分量子电路表述为Wasserstein流形上的梯度流，开发了Hierarchical DisCo-SGD和Quantum DisCo两种算法，实验表明该方法改善泛化、稳定训练并减轻量子训练中的贫瘠高原问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统统计力学学习理论将权重分布约束视为缩小解空间的限制，从而降低模型容量。作者希望采取相反的观点：将预定权重分布视为定义学习自然展开的内在几何结构，而非简单的限制条件，从而重新框架分布约束的意义。

Method: 将深度神经网络和变分量子电路表述为Wasserstein流形上的梯度流——经典网络每层对应一个经典Wasserstein空间，量子电路参数对应一个量子Wasserstein空间（使用1阶量子Wasserstein距离）。开发了层次化平均场描述，并引入两种实用算法：Hierarchical DisCo-SGD（深度网络）和Quantum DisCo（量子电路），它们遵循产品流形上的近似测地线。

Result: 在teacher-student问题、标准图像分类任务和小型变分量子分类器上的实验表明，遵循这些分布几何比无约束和纯范数基方法更能改善泛化、稳定训练，并降低量子训练中贫瘠高原的严重程度。

Conclusion: 该方法率先将结构约束重新定义为几何先验，为将生物学、频谱或硬件衍生的分布信息纳入经典和量子学习系统提供了新路径。

Abstract: Normally the statistical mechanics of learning treats constraints on weight distributions as restrictions that shrink the space of possible solutions. Therefore, it reduces model capacity. In this paper we would like to take a contrary approach, which, however, is based on the earlier work on distribution-constrained perceptrons. Rather than treating a prescribed weight distribution as a mere restriction, we propose that it defines the intrinsic geometry upon which learning naturally unfolds. We formulate both deep neural networks and variational quantum circuits as gradient flows on a product of Wasserstein manifolds -- one classical Wasserstein space for each layer and one quantum Wasserstein space for the circuit parameters. Within this geometry, the capacity reduction, which was previously associated with distributional constraints, appears as the metric structure of the constraint manifold itself. We develop a hierarchical mean-field description for deep networks, extend the framework to the quantum setting using the quantum Wasserstein distance of order 1, and introduce two such practical algorithms, Hierarchical DisCo-SGD and Quantum DisCo, that follow approximate geodesics on the manifold of the product itself. Experiments on teacher-student problems, standard image classification tasks, and small variational quantum classifiers show that respecting these distributional geometries improves generalization, stabilizes training, and reduces the severity of barren plateaus compared with unconstrained and purely norm-based baselines. This approach firstly reframes structural constraints as geometric priors and suggests a route for incorporating biological, spectral, or hardware-derived distributional information into both learning systems, viz., classical and quantum learning.

</details>


### [161] [A Trust-region Framework for Moment Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04026)
*Oluwasegun A. Somefun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个信赖域框架来理解Adam等自适应矩估计机制在随机梯度优化中的行为，推导出一族基于二阶矩估计和归一化p阶矩估计的学习率机制（称为Gmake），其中p=4时涉及峰度类估计。实验表明，当信赖域约束较弱时四阶矩实现收益最大，而随着约束增强，二阶矩实现变得更有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Adam等自适应矩估计方法在实践中被广泛使用且效果显著，但其背后的理论机制尚不清晰。本文旨在通过一个统一的信赖域框架，从理论层面解释自适应矩估计、学习率调度、动量的谱低通滤波以及算子级谱归一化等看似不同的技术之间的内在联系，为理解这些优化机制提供统一视角。

Method: 作者构建了一个信赖域框架，其中每个权重的更新步长幅度被限制在一个由p阶矩约束（p∈[2,4]）控制的信赖区域内。由此推导出一族学习率机制，基于二阶矩估计和归一化p阶矩估计；当p=4时涉及峰度估计。该一般性机制Gmake将矩估计归一化、学习率调度、作为动量的谱低通滤波、算子级谱归一化统一在同一个信赖域框架中。实验使用GPT2-124M在FineWeb-Edu和TinyStories数据集上训练进行验证。

Result: 在GPT2-124M（FineWeb-Edu和TinyStories数据集）上的实验表明：四阶矩实现在信赖域约束较弱时提供最大收益；随着引入越来越强的信赖域控制，二阶矩实现变得更有竞争力，通常在验证损失上略优于对应的四阶矩实现。

Conclusion: 本文通过信赖域框架统一解释了多种自适应优化机制，揭示了高阶矩（如四阶矩/峰度）与二阶矩在不同约束强度下的性能权衡关系，为选择合适的学习率机制提供了理论依据。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop a trust-region framework for understanding the behavior of adaptive moment estimation mechanisms, such as \textsc{Adam}, in stochastic gradient optimization. Specifically, in this framework, the magnitude of the update step for each individual weight is constrained within a trust-region governed by a moment constraint of order $p\in[2,4]$. The resulting derivation then leads to a family of learning-rate mechanisms based on second-moment estimation and a normalized $p$-th moment estimation. When $p=4$, this involves kurtosis-like estimation.
